@-webkit-keyframes rotate {
    0% {
    	-webkit-transform: rotate(0deg);
    			transform: rotate(0deg);
    }

    100% {
    	-webkit-transform: rotate(360deg);
    			transform: rotate(360deg);
    }
}

@-moz-keyframes rotate {
    0% {
    	-moz-transform: rotate(0deg);
    		 transform: rotate(0deg);
    }

    100% {
    	-moz-transform: rotate(360deg);
    		 transform: rotate(360deg);
    }
}

@-o-keyframes rotate {
    0% {
    	-o-transform: rotate(0deg);
    	   transform: rotate(0deg);
    }

    100% {
    	-o-transform: rotate(360deg);
    	   transform: rotate(360deg);
    }
}

@keyframes rotate {
    0% {
    	-webkit-transform: rotate(0deg);
    	   -moz-transform: rotate(0deg);
    		 -o-transform: rotate(0deg);
    			transform: rotate(0deg);
    }

    100% {
    	-webkit-transform: rotate(360deg);
    	   -moz-transform: rotate(360deg);
    		 -o-transform: rotate(360deg);
    			transform: rotate(360deg);
    }
}

[[html]]
<head>
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css?family=PT+Mono">
<style type="text/css">
body {
    --wght: 900;
    color: rgb(252, 252, 252);
    font-family: "PT Mono", "Courier New", Courier, monospace;
    font-size: 0.938rem;
    font-weight: 900;
    line-height: 1.406rem;
    -webkit-font-kerning: normal;
    font-kerning: normal;
    font-variant-numeric: normal;
    -webkit-font-feature-settings: "lnum", "kern", "liga", "clig", "calt";
    -moz-font-feature-settings: "lnum", "kern", "liga", "clig", "calt";
    font-feature-settings: "lnum", "kern", "liga", "clig", "calt";
    text-rendering: auto;
    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
    -moz-osx-font-smoothing: grayscale;
    orphans: 2;
    -webkit-hyphens: manual;
    -moz-hyphens: manual;
    -ms-hyphens: manual;
    hyphens: manual;
    word-break: normal
}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<span id="random_text"></span>
</body>
<script src="https://scp-wiki.wdfiles.com/local--files/fragment%3Ascp-4485-3/mersenne-twister.js"></script>
<script type="text/javascript">
if (localStorage.getItem("RandomID") === null) {
    var randomArray = [];
    var randomID = "";
    var alphabet = to_a();
    var symbol = ["/",".","|"]

    function to_a(c1 = 'a', c2 = 'z') {
    		a = 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz'.split('');
    		return (a.slice(a.indexOf(c1), a.indexOf(c2) + 1));
    }
    
    var m = new MersenneTwister();
    
    function generateId (num) {
    	console.log("num=" + num);
    	for (var i=0; i < num; i++) {
    		var randNum = Math.floor(m.random(m.random() * 100) * 100);
    		var newNum;
    		console.log("randNum=" + randNum);
    		if (0 < randNum && randNum <= 20) {
    			var n = new MersenneTwister(i);
    			newNum = alphabet[Math.floor((n.random() * 25))];
    		}
    		if (21 < randNum && randNum <= 90) {
    			var n = new MersenneTwister(i);
    			newNum = Math.floor((n.random() * 10));
    		}
    		if (91 < randNum && randNum <= 99) {
    			var n = new MersenneTwister(i);
    			newNum = symbol[Math.floor((n.random() * 3))];
    		}
    		randomArray.push(newNum);
    	}
    	randomID =  randomArray.join("");
    	return randomID;
    }
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
    	localStorage.setItem("RandomID", generateId(20));
    });
}
    
document.getElementById("random_text").innerHTML = localStorage.getItem("RandomID");
</script>
[[/html]]
[[/div]]
[[/div]]
[[/div]]
[[div_ class="warning-body"]]
Full understanding of SCP-4485 is required by all foundation employees. As such, all documents pertaining to SCP-4485 included in this database entry and a full understanding of each document is required before progress to the next series of documents is allowed. Due to the complexity of the anomaly, the documents will be displayed in chronological order. Your direct supervisor will monitor your progress and give permission to continue when satisfactory understanding is obtained.
[[/div]]

    <style>

    			max-height 1.2s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			color 1.2s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			background 1.2s 1.3s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			transform 0.2s 1.3s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			box-shadow 0.2s 1.3s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    		white-space: nowrap;
    		font-weight: 900;
    		margin: 1rem auto;
    		font-family: var(--title-font);
    		line-height: 1;
    		font-size: 1.5em;
    		text-align: center;
    		display: flex;
    		flex-wrap: wrap;
    		flex-direction: column;
    		align-items: center;
    		justify-content: center;
    		position: relative;
    		text-indent: 0;
    	}

    	.continue-button a {
    		position: absolute;
    		top: 0;
    		left: 0;
    		width: 100%;
    		height: 100%;
    		z-index: 5;
    		cursor: progress;
    	}

    	.continue-button a.active-link {
    		cursor: pointer !important;
    	}

    	.continue-button br {
    		display: none;
    	}

    	.continue-button::before {
    		pointer-events: none;
    		content: " ";

    		top: -0.625rem;
    		left: -0.625rem;
    		width: calc(100% + 1.25rem);
    		height: calc(100% + 1.25rem);
    		/*! background-color: rgb(var(--swatch-primary)); */
    		background-image: url("https://scp-wiki.wikidot.com/local--files/theme:the-foundation-theme/foundation-circles-bg.png");
    		background-size: 50vh 50vw;

    		background-position: center center;
    		z-index: -1;
    	}

    	.continue-button:hover::before {

    		z-index: -1;
    		text-indent: 0;
    	}

    	.continue-button:hover {
    		text-indent: -4em;
    		background: rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome));
    		color: rgba(var(--white-monochrome));
    		width: 17em;
    		max-height: 10em;
    		transform: translateX(0.625rem) translateY(-0.625rem);
    		box-shadow:
    			0.625rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			-0.625rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0 0.625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0 -0.625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0.625rem 0.625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			-0.625rem 0.625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0.625rem -0.625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			-0.625rem -0.625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1);
    	}

    	.continue-button:hover span {
    		color: rgba(var(--white-monochrome), 1);
    		text-shadow:
    			0.0625rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			-0.0625rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0 0.0625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0 -0.0625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0.0625rem 0.0625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			-0.0625rem 0.0625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0.0625rem -0.0625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			-0.0625rem -0.0625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1);
    		transition:
    			opacity 1.1s 1.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			transform 1.4s 1.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			color 1.4s 6.8s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			text-shadow 1.4s 6.8s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			font-size 400ms 6.8s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    		transform: translateX(0) translateY(0.5rem);
    		opacity: 1;
    		font-size: 3em;
    	}

    	.continue-button span::before {
    		content: " ";
    		pointer-events: none;
    		position: absolute;
    		top: 0;
    		left: 0;
    		width: 100%;
    		height: 100%;
    		border-radius: 50%;
    		text-indent: -3em;
    		transform: rotateZ(360deg);
    	}

    	.continue-button:hover span::before {
    		transition:
    			transform 6.8s 1500ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    		animation:
    			border 5.4s cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.6, 0.8, 1) 1500ms 1 forwards,
    			rotate 1.125s linear 1500ms 5 backwards;
    		transform: rotateZ(0deg);
    	}

    	@keyframes border {
    		0% {
    			box-shadow:
    				inset 0 0.3125rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Top 1 */
    				inset 0.234375rem 0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Top Left 2 */
    				inset 0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Left 3 */
    				inset 0 -0.3125rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom 4*/
    				inset 0.234375rem -0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom Left 5 */
    				inset -0.234375rem -0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom Right 6 */
    				inset -0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Right 7 */
    				inset -0.234375rem 0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0);
    			/* Top Right 8 */
    		}

    		6.25% {
    			box-shadow:
    				inset 0 0.3125rem 0 rgba(var(--white-monochrome)),
    				/* Top 1 */
    				inset 0.234375rem 0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Top Left 2 */
    				inset 0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Left 3 */
    				inset 0 -0.3125rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom 4*/
    				inset 0.234375rem -0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom Left 5 */
    				inset -0.234375rem -0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom Right 6 */
    				inset -0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Right 7 */
    				inset -0.234375rem 0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0);
    			/* Top Right 8 */
    		}

    		93.75% {
    			box-shadow:
    				inset 0 0.3125rem 0 rgba(var(--white-monochrome)),
    				/* Top 1 */
    				inset 0.234375rem 0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Top Left 2 */
    				inset 0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Left 3 */
    				inset 0 -0.3125rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom 4*/
    				inset 0.234375rem -0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom Left 5 */
    				inset -0.234375rem -0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom Right 6 */
    				inset -0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Right 7 */
    				inset -0.234375rem 0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--white-monochrome));
    			/* Top Right 8 */
    		}

    		100% {
    			box-shadow:
    				inset 0 0.3125rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Top 1 */
    				inset 0.234375rem 0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Top Left 2 */
    				inset 0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Left 3 */
    				inset 0 -0.3125rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom 4*/

    				/* Bottom Left 5 */

    				/* Bottom Right 6 */

    				/* Right 7 */
    				inset -0.234375rem 0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0);
    			/* Top Right 8 */

    		}
    	}

    	@keyframes rotate {
    		0% {
    			transform: rotate(0deg);
    		}

    		100% {
    			transform: rotate(360deg);

    	@media only screen and (max-width: 400px) {

    			font-size: 1em;

<body>
    <div id="continue-button-id" class="continue-button">
    	<a id="continue-link" href="https://scp-wiki.wikidot.com/scp-4485/offset/1"></a>
    	<div class="accept">ACCEPT CONDITIONS</div>
    	<div class="supervisor">SUPERVISOR APPROVAL</div>
    	<br>
    	<span>✔</span>
    </div>
    <script>
    	var continueButton = document.getElementById("continue-button-id");
    	var continueLink = document.getElementById("continue-link");
    	var to = null;

    	function mouseOver() {
    		if(to){
    			clearTimeout(to);
    		}
    		to = setTimeout(function(){
    			continueButton.classList.remove("active-hover");
    			continueLink.classList.add("active-link");
    		}, 5500);
    	}

    	function mouseOut() {
    		if(to){
    			clearTimeout(to);
    		}
    		continueLink.classList.remove("active-link");
    	}

    	continueLink.addEventListener("mouseover", mouseOver);
    	continueLink.addEventListener("mouseout", mouseOut);
    </script>
</body>

[[/html]]

.warning-container,

.warning-container {

    width: 90%;
    margin: 0 auto;

}

    display: -webkit-box;

    display: -ms-flexbox;

    -moz-box-align: center;

.report_container {
    --wght: 400;
    font-family: var(--mono-font);
    padding: 2rem 2rem 9rem 2rem;
    font-weight: 400;
    font-size: 1em;
    -webkit-border-top-right-radius: 0.2rem;
    -moz-border-radius-topright: 0.2rem;
    border-top-right-radius: 0.2rem;
    -webkit-border-bottom-right-radius: 0.25rem;
    -moz-border-radius-bottomright: 0.25rem;
    border-bottom-right-radius: 0.25rem;
    -webkit-border-top-left-radius: 0.28rem;
    -moz-border-radius-topleft: 0.28rem;
    border-top-left-radius: 0.28rem;
    -webkit-border-bottom-left-radius: 0.24rem;
    -moz-border-radius-bottomleft: 0.24rem;
    border-bottom-left-radius: 0.24rem;
    -webkit-box-shadow:
    	inset 0.125rem 0.125rem 0 rgba(var(--gray-monochrome), 1),
    	inset -0.125rem 0.125rem 0 rgba(var(--gray-monochrome), 1),
    	inset -0.125rem -0.125rem 0 rgba(var(--gray-monochrome), 1),
    	inset 0.125rem -0.125rem 0 rgba(var(--gray-monochrome), 1);
    -moz-box-shadow:
    	inset 0.125rem 0.125rem 0 rgba(var(--gray-monochrome), 1),
    	inset -0.125rem 0.125rem 0 rgba(var(--gray-monochrome), 1),
    	inset -0.125rem -0.125rem 0 rgba(var(--gray-monochrome), 1),
    	inset 0.125rem -0.125rem 0 rgba(var(--gray-monochrome), 1);
    box-shadow:
    	inset 0.125rem 0.125rem 0 rgba(var(--gray-monochrome), 1),
    	inset -0.125rem 0.125rem 0 rgba(var(--gray-monochrome), 1),
    	inset -0.125rem -0.125rem 0 rgba(var(--gray-monochrome), 1),
    	inset 0.125rem -0.125rem 0 rgba(var(--gray-monochrome), 1);
    margin: 1rem;
    border: 1rem solid rgb(var(--white-monochrome), 0);
}

.report_container,
.report_container * {
    font-variation-settings: 
    	"wght" var(--mono-wght), 
    	"slnt" var(--slnt), 
    	"CASL" var(--CASL), 
    	"CRSV" var(--CRSV), 
    	"MONO" var(--MONO);
}

div.main_container:nth-child(10) > div:nth-child(1) {
    padding: 2rem;
}

div.main_container:nth-child(1) > div {
    -webkit-transform: rotate(-0.15deg) scale(0.99);
    -moz-transform: rotate(-0.15deg) scale(0.99);
    -ms-transform: rotate(-0.15deg) scale(0.99);
    -o-transform: rotate(-0.15deg) scale(0.99);
    transform: rotate(-0.15deg) scale(0.99);
}

div.main_container:nth-child(2) > div {
    -webkit-transform: rotate(-0.25deg) scale(0.99);
    -moz-transform: rotate(-0.25deg) scale(0.99);
    -ms-transform: rotate(-0.25deg) scale(0.99);
    -o-transform: rotate(-0.25deg) scale(0.99);
    transform: rotate(-0.25deg) scale(0.99);
}

div.main_container:nth-child(3) > div {
    -webkit-transform: rotate(0.15deg) scale(0.99);
    -moz-transform: rotate(0.15deg) scale(0.99);
    -ms-transform: rotate(0.15deg) scale(0.99);
    -o-transform: rotate(0.15deg) scale(0.99);
    transform: rotate(0.15deg) scale(0.99);
}

div.main_container:nth-child(1)::before {
    -webkit-transform: rotate(5deg);
    -moz-transform: rotate(5deg);
    -ms-transform: rotate(5deg);
    -o-transform: rotate(5deg);
    transform: rotate(5deg);
}

div.main_container:nth-child(2)::before {
    -webkit-transform: rotate(2deg) translateY(-1.25rem) translateX(0.625rem);
    -moz-transform: rotate(2deg) translateY(-1.25rem) translateX(0.625rem);
    -ms-transform: rotate(2deg) translateY(-1.25rem) translateX(0.625rem);
    -o-transform: rotate(2deg) translateY(-1.25rem) translateX(0.625rem);
    transform: rotate(2deg) translateY(-1.25rem) translateX(0.625rem);
}

div.main_container:nth-child(3)::before {
    -webkit-transform: rotate(-1deg) translateY(1rem) translateX(2rem);
    -moz-transform: rotate(-1deg) translateY(1rem) translateX(2rem);
    -ms-transform: rotate(-1deg) translateY(1rem) translateX(2rem);
    -o-transform: rotate(-1deg) translateY(1rem) translateX(2rem);
    transform: rotate(-1deg) translateY(1rem) translateX(2rem);
}

[[/module]]

[[div_ class="page-title"]]
SCP-4485 ORIENTATION PART 1 OF 8
[[/div]]
@@ @@
[[div_ class="warning-container"]]
[[div_ class="warning-top"]]
!! REMOTE TERMINAL MESSAGE !!
[[/div]]
[[div class="warning-body"]]
Good Afternoon, Researcher, 

Dr. Lark and myself are pleased to see you are complying with the compulsory SCP-4485 orientation. We both would have been most displeased to have to terminate one of our most well respected and senior researchers.

It is imperative that you carefully read all documents and comprehend them to a degree that I find satisfactory. As such, I will be following your progress on a remote terminal. If it is found that you are within acceptable levels, you will be permitted to leave your office. 

Do not disappoint me.

Lloyd Michaelson
Director,  Site-12
[[/div]]
[[/div]]

------

||||~ SCP-4485 DOCUMENT ONE ||
|| Initial SCP Document || Filed 8 July, 1967 ||
[[collapsible show="⇛ CLICK TO BEGIN ⇚" hide="⤋ HIDE DOCUMENT ⤋"]]

    				[[/div]]
    				[[div_ class="cell3 empty_cell"]]
    				                                               
    				[[/div]]
    				[[div_ class="cell4 empty_cell"]]

    				[[div_ class="cell5 empty_cell"]]

    				[[/div]]
    			[[/div]]
    		[[/div]]
    	[[/div]]
    	[[div_ class="report_bottom"]]
    		[[div_ class="data-1"]]
    			[[div_ class="header"]]
    				[[div_ class="cell1"]]
    					Related Persons:
    				[[/div]]
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div_ class="row"]]
    				[[div_ class="cell1"]]
    					Max Kelper                    
    					[[div_ class="footnote_block one"]]
    						[[div_ class="footnote_num"]]
    							1  
    						[[/div]]
    						[[div_ class="footnote_content"]]
    							Known Church of the Broken God supplicant active in southern West Germany.
    						[[/div]]
    					[[/div]]
    				[[/div]]
    				[[div_ class="cell2"]]
    					Josef Von Schmetterline                    
    					[[div_ class="footnote_block two"]]
    						[[div_ class="footnote_num"]]
    							2
    						[[/div]]
    						[[div_ class="footnote_content"]]
    							Known Church of the Broken God supplicant active in southern West Germany.
    						[[/div]]
    					[[/div]]                    
    				[[/div]]
    				[[div_ class="cell2"]]
    					Boris Aldermann                    
    					[[div_ class="footnote_block three"]]
    						[[div_ class="footnote_num"]]
    							3
    						[[/div]]
    						[[div_ class="footnote_content"]]
    							Church of the Broken God regional leader in southern West Germany.
    						[[/div]]
    					[[/div]]                    
    				[[/div]]
    				[[div_ class="cell4 empty_cell"]]
    				         
    				[[/div]]
    				[[div_ class="cell5 empty_cell"]]
    				         
    				[[/div]]
    				[[div_ class="cell6 empty_cell"]]
    				         
    				[[/div]]
    				[[div_ class="cell7 empty_cell"]]
    				         
    				[[/div]]
    				[[div_ class="cell8 empty_cell"]]
    				         
    				[[/div]]
    			[[/div]]
    		[[/div]]
    	[[/div]]
    	[[div_ class="containment"]]
    		[[div_ class="report_title"]]
    			Secure Containment Protocols
    		[[/div]]

    		[[div_ class="contents"]]
    		SCP-4485 is to be stored within a high-security containment locker at Site-35. Access to SCP-4485 is restricted to those personnel who are certified by the on-site accredited logician, to be appointed by the Foundation European Regional Mathematical Studies Head (currently Dr. Viktor Jates). Prior to receiving access clearance to SCP-4485, subjects must show they have a mastery over the basic Three Laws of Thought and their application:
    		[[/div]]
    	[[/div]]
    [[/div]]
[[/div]]
[[div_ class="main_container"]]
    [[div_ class="report_container"]]
    	[[div_ class="containment"]]
    		[[div_ class="contents"]]
    		[[ul]]
    			[[li]]Principle of Identity (Each thing is identical to itself)[[/li]]
    			[[li]]Law of Noncontradiction (A proposition cannot be both true and false)[[/li]]
    			[[li]]Law of Excluded Middle (A proposition must be either true or false)[[/li]]
    		[[/ul]]

    		Additionally, subjects must show a mastery over Schopenhauer's Four Laws as established in __On the Fourfold Root of the Principle of Sufficient Reason__ and Boole's alternate algebraic representation as presented in __An Investigation on the Laws of Thought__, a thorough understanding of Russell and Whitehead's __Principia Mathematica__ and Russell's __The Problems of Philosophy__, and be able to independently reason Gödel's proof.

    		Lastly, subjects must understand and apply Tarski's adaptations to the Law of Identity (Leibniz' Law, Law of Reflexivity, Law of Symmetry, Law of Transitivity and its inverse). Subjects must also be familiar with contemporary logicism, such as intuitionistic logic, paraconsistent logic, three-valued logic, modal propositional calculi, and fuzzy logic.

    		In short, subjects must have an absolute mastery, at the highest level achievable by human beings in our current understanding of their nature, over the ability to adeptly reason what is true and logical, or false and illogical.

    		Areas affected by SCP-4485 are to be quarantined and guarded by Foundation agents.
    		[[/div]]
    	[[/div]]
    [[/div]]
[[/div]]
[[div_ class="main_container"]]
    [[div_ class="report_container"]]
    	[[div_ class="description"]]
    		[[div_ class="report_title"]]
    			Description:
    		[[/div]]    
    		[[div_ class="contents"]]
    			SCP-4485 is a small leatherbound book written by German-French artist Jean Arp (Hans Arp            
    			[[div_ class="footnote_block four"]]
    				[[div_ class="footnote_num"]]
    					4  
    				[[/div]]
    				[[div_ class="footnote_content"]]
    					While the artist's given name under French law was Jean, he continued to refer to himself by his German name, Hans, when speaking German.
    				[[/div]]
    			[[/div]]            
    			). SCP-4485 itself is unremarkable in appearance, being moderately worn and slightly faded. The name of the author, Hans Arp, is pressed into the leather on the back of the journal in block capitalization. Within the front cover of SCP-4485 are the words "gott ist ohne zweck"            
    			[[div_ class="footnote_block five"]]
    				[[div_ class="footnote_num"]]
    					5
    				[[/div]]
    				[[div_ class="footnote_content"]]
    					"god is without purpose"
    				[[/div]]
    			[[/div]]            
    			beneath a crude drawing of a fish.

    			SCP-4485 has an acute, adverse affect on reality and human cognition. Individuals who are exposed to SCP-4485 and do not have proper training in logicism will become disoriented and unable to differentiate between truths and falsehoods. This inability to identify what is logical and illogical manifests physically as alterations to the structural makeup of reality. The area directly surrounding SCP-4485 will begin to degrade over time, becoming increasingly disorderly and nonsensical. These effects on reality linger even after the removal of SCP-4485. Currently there is no means by which these alterations can be reversed.
    		[[/div]]
    	[[/div]]
    	[[div_ class="discovery"]]
    		[[div_ class="contents"]]
    			[[span class="discovery_label"]]Discovery:[[/span]] SCP-4485 was discovered in the town of
    			[[div_ class="nowrap"]]
    				Bramund            
    				[[div_ class="footnote_block six"]]
    					[[div_ class="footnote_num"]]
    						6  
    					[[/div]]
    					[[div_ class="footnote_content"]]
    					Since struck from public records.
    					[[/div]]
    				[[/div]] 
    			[[/div]]    
    			after reports of increased mental illness in the region, as well as other unusual phenomena. Additional information pertaining to SCP-4485's discovery is available in Agent Norman's report contained within this file.
    		[[/div]]
    	[[/div]]
    [[/div]]
[[/div]]
[[/collapsible]]
------

||||~ SCP-4485 DOCUMENT TWO ||
|| Field Agent Norman's Report || Filed 29 June, 1967 ||
[[collapsible show="⇛ CLICK TO BEGIN ⇚" hide="⤋ HIDE DOCUMENT ⤋"]]
[[div_ class="main_container"]]
    [[div_ class="report_container"]]
    	[[div_ class="report_top"]]
    		[[div_ class="header"]]
    			[[div_ class="cell1"]]
    				TOP SECRET
    			[[/div]]
    		[[/div]]
    		[[div_ class="stamp"]]
    			[[div_ class="cell1"]]
    				[[span]]Approved for use in training. Clearance Level 4.[[/span]]
    				[[span class="sui_sign"]]Dr. Chen Sui[[/span]]
    			[[/div]]
    		[[/div]]
    	[[/div]]
    	[[div_ class="fiend_report"]]
    		[[div_ class="report_title"]]
    			[[span]]Foundation Field Report[[/span]]
    			[[span]][[span]]Agent Designation:[[/span]] Agent Fredrick Norman[[/span]]
    		[[/div]]
    		[[div_ class="contents"]]
[[span]]Forward:[[/span]] The report of SCP Foundation field agent Fredrick Norman, assigned to Site-35, in the matter of the SCP-4485 anomaly.

[[span]]Report Proper:[[/span]] Initial communication with field agent [[span class="black_highlighter"]]██████[[/span]] [[span class="black_highlighter"]]█████[[/span]] proved substantial enough evidence to move operations to Bramund and discover the source of the abnormality. Subjects admitted to local hospital stated disorientation and decreased motivation. The subjects I reviewed did not appear physically unusual, however, each of them seemed unable to differentiate between hallucinations and reality, though these hallucinations did not seem to trouble them in any way. Subjects would occasionally mutter gibberish or laugh randomly without explanation, and seldom responded to questioning.

Two subjects, Max Kelper and Josef Von Schmetterline were known actives within the major Prussian Church of the Broken God sect and, during our most recent encounter, had been hostile zealots. The hostility appeared to have left them, and they did not respond to questioning about their pasts, saying only that "god is already here" in reference to the anomalous activity within Bramund. Both men chattered like animals the entire time I was with them, and continued to do so after I had departed.
    		[[/div]]
    	[[/div]]
    [[/div]]
[[/div]]

[[div_ class="main_container"]]
    [[div_ class="report_container"]]
    	[[div_ class="fiend_report"]]
    		[[div_ class="contents"]]
All subjects we interviewed at the [[span class="black_highlighter"]]███████████[[/span]] [[span class="black_highlighter"]]█████████[[/span]] Hospital no longer appeared to recognize their own names, referring to themselves and each other as "hansarp". They provided no elaboration as to the meaning of this name or its significance as it pertains to their affliction.

After three days stay at the hospital, our team moved in to Bramund while additional resources were called to transfer the afflicted to Site-35 for examination. It was agreed by all that the subjects were experiencing unusual enough symptoms as to warrant classification and study. We arrived at Grenwald, near Bramund across the river, on June 19th. It was our intention to collect our resources at Grenwald and enter Bramund on the upcoming Sunday; however, activity within the Bramund region warranted an expedited response.

Locals within Grenwald began to complain about the texture of the sky over Bramund. I was unable to make this distinction, but was easily able to see the color change. Where before had been blue skies with the mountains behind was now clearly various shades of grey and orange, in a solid column extending up from the city's borders. Strange forms had begun to appear within the column - geometric shapes that were difficult to observe, rippling, even symbols of some strange alphabet I did not recognize. Realizing this was an escalation in expected activity, we mobilized within the hour and moved to Bramund.

There was a distinct haze on the road to the city that grew thicker as we approached. The column of greyscale we had seen in Grenwald grew more and more difficult to see, and at times it was impossible to see the sky at all. Several other members of our accompanying task force described feeling uncertain about the path we were taking, despite us clearly being on the road. On several occasions some men stopped and declared we were lost, and needed
    		[[/div]]
    	[[/div]]
    [[/div]]
[[/div]]

[[div_ class="main_container"]]
    [[div_ class="report_container"]]
    	[[div_ class="fiend_report"]]
    		[[div_ class="contents"]]
to return to Grenwald. The task force commander lost track of how many men were assigned to his unit. [[span class="black_highlighter"]]█████[[/span]] [[span class="black_highlighter"]]██████[[/span]] described how at one point, three of the men seemed to simply stop walking, turn 90 degrees to the right, and walk off the road, though I did not see this myself.

It became clear when we had passed into Bramund, as the color had come out of our equipment and our skin. With the exception of the occasional orange coloration, there was no other color to be seen at all. There was a strange sort of cycling wind within the cylinder, that passed us every so often and seemed to carry sound with it. This impeded our ability to communicate with each other, and we were forced to resort to visual communication in order to proceed.

The state of affairs within Bramund was not exceptional. Some structures were seemingly disassembled and hanging in the air just off their foundations, as if gravity had ceased to operate in their vicinity. Large, dark shapes were somehow visible beneath the earth moving around like sharks circling prey, but did not ever surface. From somewhere else in the fog we could hear the sound of strange music every time the wind passed over us, and proceeded towards the source. We were impeded by a panic that set over the men due to some additional affliction that I did not seem to recognize, and several were left behind to be taken out of the city by their comrades.

The epicenter of activity was the remains of a small library. I recognized the structure immediately as the former gathering place of local anartists, prior to a raid that saw several of them taken into custody and the structure demolished. Within the ruins was a grisly sight; a circle of living human beings, twisted into strange and unnatural shapes, seemingly part of a large, complex machine in the center of the main room. 
    		[[/div]]
    	[[/div]]
    [[/div]]
[[/div]]

[[div_ class="main_container"]]
    [[div_ class="report_container"]]
    	[[div_ class="fiend_report"]]
    		[[div_ class="contents"]]
As the machine's action turned under some strange power, it would raise and drop a large mass of barbed steel, concrete, and human remains onto a platform. Each time this action was taken, the circle of humans would proceed forward, twisting and dancing and singing a haunting and unintelligible harmony with the machine's grotesque melody. The next human would stand on the platform, and the machine would drop, and their remains would be flung out into the air, rising unnaturally into the sky where, above the remains of the library, a strange image was forming - something that defied understanding. The clicking machine would raise again like the pendulum of a clock, another human would step onto the platform, and the process would continue.

We killed all the afflicted within the library and put the ruins to the torch. After the aberration in the sky had cleared we began to disassemble the machine within it, tearing apart what we could and using explosives for what remained. At its center we found a desiccated human man, the skin on his face peeled back and suspended away from the raw meat of his skull by wires. He was breathing. He spoke only a charred whisper through those skinless lips, but he answered when I spoke. I asked him what his name was, and he said "hansarp". I asked what had happened to him, and he handed me the book. When I asked him what it was, he told me it was god.

SCP-4485 was remanded into the custody of site containment officials and we set up a quarantine around the affected area. Forensics later informed me that this body was Boris Aldermann, the leader of the regional CotBG sect. We were not able to remove Aldermann's body from the machine, and I believe it is still there.
    		[[/div]]
    	[[/div]]
    [[/div]]
[[/div]]
[[/collapsible]]

------

[[div_ class="warning-container"]]
[[html]]
<!DOCTYPE html>

<head>
    <base target="_parent">
    <meta charset="utf-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
    <meta http-equiv="X-UA-Compatible" content="IE=edge">
    <link rel="stylesheet" type="text/css" href="https://d3g0gp89917ko0.cloudfront.net/v--b23e476b7ade/common--theme/base/css/style.css">
    <link rel="stylesheet" type="text/css" href="https://nu-scptheme.github.io/Black-Highlighter/css/min/black-highlighter.min.css">
    <link rel="stylesheet" type="text/css" href="https://scp-wiki.wdfiles.com/local--code/theme%3Athe-foundation-theme/1">
    <style>
    	html#html-block-html, 
    	html#html-block-html body {
    		display: flex;
    		align-items: center;
    		justify-content: center;
    		height: 100% !important;
    		width: 100% !important;
    	}
    	.continue-button {
    		--wght: 900;
    		border: 0.313rem solid transparent;
    		background: rgba(var(--swatch-primary-darkest));
    		color: rgba(var(--white-monochrome));
    		padding: 0.95em 1em;
    		width: 15em;
    		max-height: 4em;
    		transition:
    			text-indent 1.2s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			width 1.2s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			max-height 1.2s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			color 1.2s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			background 1.2s 1.3s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			transform 0.2s 1.3s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			box-shadow 0.2s 1.3s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    		white-space: nowrap;
    		font-weight: 900;
    		margin: 1rem auto;
    		font-family: var(--title-font);
    		line-height: 1;
    		font-size: 1.5em;
    		text-align: center;
    		display: flex;
    		flex-wrap: wrap;
    		flex-direction: column;
    		align-items: center;
    		justify-content: center;
    		position: relative;
    		text-indent: 0;
    	}

    	.continue-button a {
    		position: absolute;
    		top: 0;
    		left: 0;
    		width: 100%;
    		height: 100%;
    		z-index: 5;
    		cursor: progress;
    	}

    	.continue-button a.active-link {
    		cursor: pointer !important;
    	}

    	.continue-button br {
    		display: none;
    	}

    	.continue-button::before {
    		pointer-events: none;
    		content: " ";
    		position: absolute;
    		top: -0.625rem;
    		left: -0.625rem;
    		width: calc(100% + 1.25rem);
    		height: calc(100% + 1.25rem);
    		background-color: rgb(var(--swatch-primary));
    		background-image: url("https://scp-wiki.wikidot.com/local--files/theme:the-foundation-theme/foundation-circles-bg.png");
    		background-size: 50%;
    		background-attachment: fixed;
    		opacity: 0;
    		transition:
    			opacity 1.2s 1.3s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			background-size 1.2s 1.3s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    		background-position: center center;
    		z-index: -1;
    	}

    	.continue-button:hover::before {
    		opacity: 1;
    		background-size: 100%;
    	}

    	.continue-button .supervisor {
    		max-height: 0;
    		font-size: 0.85em;
    		text-align: center;
    		display: flex;
    		align-items: center;
    		justify-content: center;
    		opacity: 0;
    		transition:
    			opacity 1.1s 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			max-height 1.1s 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    	}

    	.continue-button:hover .supervisor {
    		opacity: 1;
    		max-height: 2em;
    		transition:
    			opacity 1.1s 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			max-height 1.1s 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    	}

    	.continue-button span {
    		--wght: 400;
    		display: inline-flex;
    		transform: translateX(13rem) translateY(0.5rem);
    		font-weight: 400;
    		color: rgba(var(--white-monochrome), 0);
    		opacity: 0;
    		transition:
    			opacity 1.1s 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			transform 1.1s 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			color 1.4s 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			text-shadow 1.4s 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			font-size 0s 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    		position: absolute;
    		right: 1.875rem;
    		top: 0.367rem;
    		font-size: 0em;
    		border-radius: 50%;
    		background-color: darkgreen;
    		width: 3.750rem;
    		height: 3.750rem;
    		align-items: flex-start;
    		justify-content: flex-start;
    		text-shadow:
    			0 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1);
    		z-index: -1;
    		text-indent: 0;
    	}

    	.continue-button:hover {
    		text-indent: -4em;
    		background: rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome));
    		color: rgba(var(--white-monochrome));
    		width: 17em;
    		max-height: 10em;
    		transform: translateX(0.625rem) translateY(-0.625rem);
    		box-shadow:
    			0.625rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			-0.625rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0 0.625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0 -0.625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0.625rem 0.625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			-0.625rem 0.625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0.625rem -0.625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			-0.625rem -0.625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1);
    	}

    	.continue-button:hover span {
    		color: rgba(var(--white-monochrome), 1);
    		text-shadow:
    			0.0625rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			-0.0625rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0 0.0625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0 -0.0625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0.0625rem 0.0625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			-0.0625rem 0.0625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0.0625rem -0.0625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			-0.0625rem -0.0625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1);
    		transition:
    			opacity 1.1s 1.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			transform 1.4s 1.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			color 1.4s 6.8s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			text-shadow 1.4s 6.8s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			font-size 400ms 6.8s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    		transform: translateX(0) translateY(0.5rem);
    		opacity: 1;
    		font-size: 3em;
    	}

    	.continue-button span::before {
    		content: " ";
    		pointer-events: none;
    		position: absolute;
    		top: 0;
    		left: 0;
    		width: 100%;
    		height: 100%;
    		border-radius: 50%;
    		text-indent: -3em;
    		transform: rotateZ(360deg);
    	}

    	.continue-button:hover span::before {
    		transition:
    			transform 6.8s 1500ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    		animation:
    			border 5.4s cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.6, 0.8, 1) 1500ms 1 forwards,
    			rotate 1.125s linear 1500ms 5 backwards;
    		transform: rotateZ(0deg);
    	}

    	@keyframes border {
    		0% {
    			box-shadow:
    				inset 0 0.3125rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Top 1 */
    				inset 0.234375rem 0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Top Left 2 */
    				inset 0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Left 3 */
    				inset 0 -0.3125rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom 4*/
    				inset 0.234375rem -0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom Left 5 */
    				inset -0.234375rem -0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom Right 6 */
    				inset -0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Right 7 */
    				inset -0.234375rem 0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0);
    			/* Top Right 8 */
    		}

    		6.25% {
    			box-shadow:
    				inset 0 0.3125rem 0 rgba(var(--white-monochrome)),
    				/* Top 1 */
    				inset 0.234375rem 0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Top Left 2 */
    				inset 0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Left 3 */
    				inset 0 -0.3125rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom 4*/
    				inset 0.234375rem -0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom Left 5 */
    				inset -0.234375rem -0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom Right 6 */
    				inset -0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Right 7 */
    				inset -0.234375rem 0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0);
    			/* Top Right 8 */
    		}

    		93.75% {
    			box-shadow:
    				inset 0 0.3125rem 0 rgba(var(--white-monochrome)),
    				/* Top 1 */
    				inset 0.234375rem 0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Top Left 2 */
    				inset 0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Left 3 */
    				inset 0 -0.3125rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom 4*/
    				inset 0.234375rem -0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom Left 5 */
    				inset -0.234375rem -0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom Right 6 */
    				inset -0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Right 7 */
    				inset -0.234375rem 0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--white-monochrome));
    			/* Top Right 8 */
    		}

    		100% {
    			box-shadow:
    				inset 0 0.3125rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Top 1 */
    				inset 0.234375rem 0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Top Left 2 */
    				inset 0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Left 3 */
    				inset 0 -0.3125rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom 4*/
    				inset 0.234375rem -0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom Left 5 */
    				inset -0.234375rem -0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom Right 6 */
    				inset -0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Right 7 */
    				inset -0.234375rem 0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0);
    			/* Top Right 8 */

    		}
    	}

    	@keyframes rotate {
    		0% {
    			transform: rotate(0deg);
    		}

    		100% {
    			transform: rotate(360deg);
    		}
    	}
    	@media only screen and (max-width: 400px) {
    		.continue-button {
    			font-size: 1em;
    		}
    	}

    </style>
</head>

<body>
    <div id="continue-button-id" class="continue-button">
    	<a id="continue-link" href="https://scp-wiki.wikidot.com/scp-4485/offset/2"></a>
    	<div class="accept">CLICK TO SUBMIT APPROVAL</div>
    	<div class="supervisor">SUPERVISOR APPROVAL</div>
    	<br>
    	<span>✔</span>
    </div>
    <script>
    	var continueButton = document.getElementById("continue-button-id");  
    	var continueLink = document.getElementById("continue-link"); 
    	var to = null;     

    	function mouseOver() {
    		if(to){
    			clearTimeout(to);
    		}           
    		to = setTimeout(function(){                 
    			continueButton.classList.remove("active-hover");
    			continueLink.classList.add("active-link");                    
    		}, 5500);
    	}

    	function mouseOut() {
    		if(to){
    			clearTimeout(to);
    		}
    		continueLink.classList.remove("active-link");
    	}        

    	continueLink.addEventListener("mouseover", mouseOver);
    	continueLink.addEventListener("mouseout", mouseOut);
    </script>
</body>

[[/html]]
[[/div]]

[[module css]]
:root {

}

     linear-gradient(0deg,

}

[[module css]]
@import url("https://use.typekit.net/put0nby.css");

}

.page-title {

    display: -ms-flexbox;

    width: calc(100% - 1em - 0.5rem);
    -webkit-box-align: center;
    -webkit-align-items: center;

    -ms-flex-align: center;

    -webkit-box-pack: center;

}

.page-title::before {

    left: 0;

}

.warning-container,
.warning-container * {
    -webkit-box-sizing: border-box;

}

    width: 90%;
    margin: 0 auto;

}

.warning-container .warning-top {

    display: -webkit-box;

    display: flex;

}

}

div.database_container,
div.database_container * {
    -webkit-box-sizing: border-box;
    -moz-box-sizing: border-box;
    box-sizing: border-box;
}


div.database_container {
    width: 100%;
    background-color: rgb(var(--swatch-primary-darkest));
    color: rgb(var(--white-monochrome));
    position: relative;
}

div.database_main {
    background-color: rgb(var(--black-monochrome), 0.5);
    margin: 1rem;
    padding: 1rem;
    display: -ms-grid;
    display: grid;
    	grid-template-areas:
    	"title"
    	"top"
    	"main";
    -ms-grid-columns: 100%;
    grid-template-columns: 100%;
    position: relative;
    left: 0;
    overflow-wrap: break-word;
    word-wrap: break-word;
    -webkit-hyphens: auto;
    -ms-hyphens: auto;
    -moz-hyphens: auto;
    hyphens: auto;
}

div.database_main .cell[data-label] {
    padding: 2.55em 0.5em 0.5em 0.5em;
    border: 0.0625rem #CCCCCC solid;
    border: 0.0625rem rgb(var(--swatch-border-color)) solid;
    position: relative;
}

div.database_main .cell[data-label]::before {
    --wght: 900;
    content: attr(data-label) "\A";
    -webkit-hyphens: none;
    -moz-hyphens: none;
    -ms-hyphens: none;
    hyphens: none;
    /* label + newline */
    white-space: normal;
    /* makes newline work */
    font-family: var(--mono-font);
    font-variation-settings: 
    	"wght" var(--mono-wght), 
    	"slnt" var(--slnt), 
    	"CASL" var(--CASL), 
    	"CRSV" var(--CRSV), 
    	"MONO" var(--MONO);
    display: -webkit-box;
    display: -webkit-flex;
    display: -moz-box;
    display: -ms-flexbox;
    display: flex;
    -webkit-box-align: center;
    -webkit-align-items: center;
    -moz-box-align: center;
    -ms-flex-align: center;
    align-items: center;
    -webkit-box-pack: start;
    -webkit-justify-content: flex-start;
    -moz-box-pack: start;
    -ms-flex-pack: start;
    justify-content: flex-start;
    font-size: 1em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    width: -webkit-calc(100% - 0.7em);
    width: -moz-calc(100% - 0.7em);
    width: calc(100% - 0.7em);
    position: absolute;
    margin-bottom: 1em;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    background: #aaa;
    background: rgb(var(--swatch-primary-darkest));
    color: #fcfcfc;
    color: rgb(var(--swatch-menutxt-light-color));
    padding-left: 0.7em;
    padding-top: 0.35em;
    padding-bottom: 0.3em;
    font-weight: 900;
}

div.database_main > .database_title {
    --wght: 900;
    -ms-grid-row: 1;
    -ms-grid-column: 1;
    grid-area: title;
    display: -webkit-box;
    display: -webkit-flex;
    display: -moz-box;
    display: -ms-flexbox;
    display: flex;
    font-size: 2em;
    font-weight: 900;
    text-align: center;
    width: 100%;
    -webkit-box-align: center;
    -webkit-align-items: center;
    -moz-box-align: center;
    -ms-flex-align: center;
    align-items: center;
    -webkit-box-pack: center;
    -webkit-justify-content: center;
    -moz-box-pack: center;
    -ms-flex-pack: center;
    justify-content: center;
    font-family: var(--header-font);
    letter-spacing: 0.025em;
    background: rgb(var(--swatch-primary-darkest));
    -webkit-border-top-right-radius: 0.5rem;
    -moz-border-radius-topright: 0.5rem;
    border-top-right-radius: 0.5rem;
    -webkit-border-top-left-radius: 0.5rem;
    -moz-border-radius-topleft: 0.5rem;
    border-top-left-radius: 0.5rem;
    color: rgb(var(--white-monochrome));
    border-bottom: 0.5rem double;
    margin-top: 0.5rem;
}

div.database_main > .database_table.top {
    -ms-grid-row: 2;
    -ms-grid-column: 1;
    grid-area: top;
    display: -ms-grid;
    display: grid;
    	grid-template-areas:
    	"group-name group-name"
    	"aliases logotype"
    	"leadership logotype";
    -ms-grid-columns: -webkit-calc(100% / 3) -webkit-calc((100% / 3) * 2);
    -ms-grid-columns: -moz-calc(100% / 3) -moz-calc((100% / 3) * 2);
    -ms-grid-columns: calc(100% / 3) calc((100% / 3) * 2);
    grid-template-columns: -webkit-calc(100% / 3) -webkit-calc((100% / 3) * 2);
    grid-template-columns: -moz-calc(100% / 3) -moz-calc((100% / 3) * 2);
    grid-template-columns: calc(100% / 3) calc((100% / 3) * 2);
    padding: 1rem;
    background-color: rgba(var(--black-monochrome));
}


div.database_main > .database_table.top > .cell:nth-of-type(1) {
    --wght: 900;
    -ms-grid-row: 1;
    -ms-grid-column: 1;
    -ms-grid-column-span: 2;
    grid-area: group-name;
    -ms-grid-column: 1;
    -ms-grid-column-span: 2;
    grid-column: 1 / span 2;
    font-size: 2rem;
    text-align: center;
    padding: 2.3rem 0.5rem 0.5rem 0.5rem;
    font-weight: 900;
}

div.database_main > .database_table.top > .cell:nth-of-type(1)::before {
    -webkit-box-pack: center;
    -webkit-justify-content: center;
    -moz-box-pack: center;
    -ms-flex-pack: center;
    justify-content: center;
    font-size: 1rem;
    padding: 0.35rem 0.7rem 0.3rem;
    width: -webkit-calc(100% - 1.4rem);
    width: -moz-calc(100% - 1.4rem);
    width: calc(100% - 1.4rem);
}

div.database_main > .database_table.top > .cell:nth-of-type(2) {
    -ms-grid-row: 2;
    -ms-grid-column: 1;
    grid-area: aliases;
    -ms-grid-column: 1;
    -ms-grid-column-span: 1;
    grid-column: 1 / span 1;
}

div.database_main > .database_table.top > .cell:nth-of-type(3) {
    -ms-grid-row: 3;
    -ms-grid-column: 1;
    grid-area: leadership;
    -ms-grid-column: 1;
    -ms-grid-column-span: 1;
    grid-column: 1 / span 1;
}

div.database_main > .database_table.top > .hansarp_logo {
    -ms-grid-row: 2;
    -ms-grid-row-span: 2;
    -ms-grid-column: 2;
    height: 3rem;
    grid-area: logotype;
    display: -ms-grid;
    display: grid;
    	grid-template-areas:
    	"image caption"
    	"image caption";
    -ms-grid-column: 2;
    grid-column: 2;
    height: 100%;
    width: auto;
    margin-left: 2rem;
}

div.database_main > .database_table.top > .hansarp_logo > .cell:first-of-type {
    -ms-grid-row: 1;
    -ms-grid-row-span: 2;
    -ms-grid-column: 1;
    grid-area: image;
    display: -webkit-box;
    display: -webkit-flex;
    display: -moz-box;
    display: -ms-flexbox;
    display: flex;
    position: relative;
    width: 9rem;
    height: 9rem;
    justify-self: end;
    border: 0.5rem double rgba(var(--white-monochrome));
}

div.database_main > .database_table.top > .hansarp_logo > .cell:first-of-type > img {
    background-color: rgba(var(--white-monochrome), 0.85);
    padding: 0.5em;
}

div.database_main > .database_table.top > .hansarp_logo > .cell:last-of-type {
    --wght: 900;
    -ms-grid-row: 1;
    -ms-grid-row-span: 2;
    -ms-grid-column: 2;
    display: -webkit-box;
    display: -webkit-flex;
    display: -moz-box;
    display: -ms-flexbox;
    display: flex;
    -webkit-box-align: center;
    -webkit-align-items: center;
    -moz-box-align: center;
    -ms-flex-align: center;
    align-items: center;
    grid-area: caption;
    height: 5rem;
    justify-self: start;
    -webkit-align-self: center;
    -ms-flex-item-align: center;
    align-self: center;
    padding: 1em 1em 1em 4em;
    margin-left: -3em;
    background-color: rgb(var(--swatch-primary-darkest));
    font-family: var(--mono-font);
    font-size: 1rem;
    font-weight: 900;
    hyphens: none;
}

div.database_main > .database_table.top > .hansarp_logo > .cell:last-of-type,
div.database_main > .database_table.top > .hansarp_logo > .cell:last-of-type * {
    font-variation-settings: 
    	"wght" var(--mono-wght), 
    	"slnt" var(--slnt), 
    	"CASL" var(--CASL), 
    	"CRSV" var(--CRSV), 
    	"MONO" var(--MONO);
}

div.database_main > .database_table.main {
    -ms-grid-row: 3;
    -ms-grid-column: 1;
    grid-area: main;
    display: -ms-grid;
    display: grid;
}

div.database_main > .database_table.main > .locations {
    display: -ms-grid;
    display: grid;
    	grid-template-areas:
    	"primary addtl limited";
    -ms-grid-columns: 40% 40% 20%;
    grid-template-columns: 40% 40% 20%;
    margin-top: 1rem;
}

div.database_main > .database_table.main > .locations > .cell {
    position: relative;
}

div.database_main > .database_table.main > .locations > .cell::before {
    -webkit-box-pack: center;
    -webkit-justify-content: center;
    -moz-box-pack: center;
    -ms-flex-pack: center;
    justify-content: center;
    font-size: 1rem;
    padding: 0.35rem 0.7rem 0.3rem;
    width: -webkit-calc(100% - 1.4rem);
    width: -moz-calc(100% - 1.4rem);
    width: calc(100% - 1.4rem);
    text-align: center;
}

div.database_main > .database_table.main > .locations > .cell:nth-of-type(1) {
    -ms-grid-row: 1;
    -ms-grid-column: 1;
    grid-area: primary;
}

div.database_main > .database_table.main > .locations > .cell:nth-of-type(2) {
    -ms-grid-row: 1;
    -ms-grid-column: 2;
    grid-area: addtl;
}

div.database_main > .database_table.main > .locations > .cell:nth-of-type(3) {
    -ms-grid-row: 1;
    -ms-grid-column: 3;
    grid-area: limited;
}

div.database_main > .database_table.main > .locations > .cell > ul {
    list-style: none;
    list-style-position: outside;
    display: -webkit-box;
    display: -webkit-flex;
    display: -moz-box;
    display: -ms-flexbox;
    display: flex;
    -webkit-flex-wrap: wrap;
    -ms-flex-wrap: wrap;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    position: relative;
    margin: 1.5rem 0 0 0;
    padding: 0;
}

div.database_main > .database_table.main > .locations > .cell > ul > li {
    width: 100%;
    padding: 0.25rem 0 0.25rem 0.25rem;
}

div.database_main > .database_table.main > .locations > .cell > ul > li:not(:first-of-type) {
    border-top: 0.125rem dashed rgba(var(--swatch-primary))
}

div.database_main .main_content {
    margin-top: 1rem;
    background-color: rgba(var(--swatch-primary), 0.15);
}

div.database_main .main_content .cell::before {
    -webkit-box-pack: center;
    -webkit-justify-content: center;
    -moz-box-pack: center;
    -ms-flex-pack: center;
    justify-content: center;
    font-size: 1rem;
    padding: 0.35rem 0.7rem 0.3rem;
    width: -webkit-calc(100% - 1.4rem);
    width: -moz-calc(100% - 1.4rem);
    width: calc(100% - 1.4rem);
    text-align: center;
}

div.database_main .main_content .cell div {
    font-size: 1.1rem;
    line-height: 1.75;
    padding: 0 2em;
}

/* Doc 4 Artifacts */

div.collapsible-block:nth-child(5) > div:nth-child(2) > div:nth-child(2) > p:nth-child(1) {
    margin: 0;
}

.responsive_table {
    background-color: rgba(var(--swatch-primary-darkest));
    padding: 1rem;
}

.responsive_table br {
    display: none;
}

.responsive_table .table_data,
.responsive_table .table_header_data {
    vertical-align: top;
    text-align: left;
    margin: -0.0625rem 0 0 -0.0625rem;
    position: relative;
}

.responsive_table .table_row {
    display: -ms-grid;
    display: grid;
    	grid-template-areas:
    	"ID  Title Title Title "
    	"Date Subject Subject Subject "
    	"Location Subject Subject Subject "
    	"Current Current Current Current ";
    -ms-grid-columns: 25% 75%;
    grid-template-columns: 25% 75%;
    padding: 0.375em;
    border: 0.0625rem #CCCCCC solid;
    border: 0.0625rem rgb(var(--swatch-border-color)) solid;
    margin-bottom: 1.250em;
    position: relative;
    background-color: rgba(var(--black-monochrome),0.5);
}

.responsive_table .table_data {
    padding: 1.95em 0.5em 0.5em 0.5em;
    border: 0.0625rem #CCCCCC solid;
    border: 0.0625rem rgb(var(--swatch-border-color)) solid;
    position: relative;
    background-color: rgb(var(--white-monochrome));
}

.responsive_table .table_row div:nth-of-type(1) {
    -ms-grid-row: 1;
    -ms-grid-column: 1;
    grid-area: ID;
}

.responsive_table .table_row div:nth-of-type(4) {
    -ms-grid-row: 3;
    -ms-grid-column: 1;
    grid-area: Location;
}

.responsive_table .table_row div:nth-of-type(2) {
    -ms-grid-row: 2;
    -ms-grid-column: 1;
    grid-area: Date;
}

.responsive_table .table_row div:nth-of-type(3) {
    -ms-grid-row: 1;
    -ms-grid-column: 2;
    -ms-grid-column-span: 3;
    grid-area: Title;
}

.responsive_table .table_row div:nth-of-type(5) {
    -ms-grid-row: 2;
    -ms-grid-row-span: 2;
    -ms-grid-column: 2;
    -ms-grid-column-span: 3;
    grid-area: Subject;
}

.responsive_table .table_row div:nth-of-type(6) {
    -ms-grid-row: 4;
    -ms-grid-column: 1;
    -ms-grid-column-span: 4;
    grid-area: Current;
}

/* Add labels */
.responsive_table .table_data::before {
    --wght: 700;
    content: attr(data-label) "\A";
    /* label + newline */
    white-space: pre;
    /* makes newline work */
    font-size: 0.750em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    width: -webkit-calc(100% - 0.7em);
    width: -moz-calc(100% - 0.7em);
    width: calc(100% - 0.7em);
    height: 1.35em;
    position: absolute;
    margin-bottom: 1em;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    background: #aaa;
    background: rgb(var(--swatch-primary-darkest));
    color: #fcfcfc;
    color: rgb(var(--swatch-menutxt-light-color));
    padding-left: 0.7em;
    padding-top: 0.35em;
    padding-bottom: 0.7em;
    font-weight: 700;
}


/* Doc 5 Interview */

:root {
    --jates-color: 34, 31, 71;
    --harmon-color: 142, 101, 61;
    --fitzwell-color: 31, 71, 56;
}

.interview-foreword {
    --wght: 600;
    margin: 1rem;
    font-size: 1.1rem;
    line-height: 1.25;
    padding: 0.5rem;
    background: rgba(var(--gray-monochrome), 0.15);
    font-weight: 600;
    font-family: var(--mono-font);
}

.interview-foreword,
.interview-foreword * {
    font-variation-settings: 
    	"wght" var(--mono-wght), 
    	"slnt" var(--slnt), 
    	"CASL" var(--CASL), 
    	"CRSV" var(--CRSV), 
    	"MONO" var(--MONO);
}

.interview-container {
    background-color: rgba(var(--gray-monochrome), 0.5);
    padding: 1rem;
}

.interview-container > .on-call {
    background-color: rgb(var(--very-light-gray-monochrome), 1);
    padding: 1rem 0.5em 0em 0.5em;
    border: 0.0625rem #CCCCCC solid;
    border: 0.0625rem rgb(var(--swatch-border-color)) solid;
    position: relative;
}

.interview-container > .on-call::before {
    --wght: 900;
    content: " ";
    -webkit-hyphens: none;
    -moz-hyphens: none;
    -ms-hyphens: none;
    hyphens: none;
    /* label + newline */
    white-space: normal;
    /* makes newline work */
    font-family: var(--title-font);
    display: -webkit-box;
    display: -webkit-flex;
    display: -moz-box;
    display: -ms-flexbox;
    display: flex;
    -webkit-box-align: center;
    -webkit-align-items: center;
       -moz-box-align: center;
    	-ms-flex-align: center;
    		align-items: center;
    -webkit-box-pack: center;
    -webkit-justify-content: center;
       -moz-box-pack: center;
    	-ms-flex-pack: center;
    		justify-content: center;
    font-size: 1em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    width: 100%;
    position: absolute;
    margin-bottom: 1em;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    background: #aaa;
    background: rgb(var(--swatch-primary-darkest));
    color: #fcfcfc;
    color: rgb(var(--swatch-menutxt-light-color));
    padding-top: 0.35em;
    padding-bottom: 0.3em;
    font-weight: 900;
    font-size: 1.25em;
}

.interview-container > .on-call::before {
    content: "On-Call"
}

.interview-container > .on-call > ul {
    padding: 0.25rem;
    display: -ms-grid;
    display: grid;
    -ms-grid-columns: -webkit-calc(100% /3) -webkit-calc(100% /3) -webkit-calc(100% /3);
    -ms-grid-columns: -moz-calc(100% /3) -moz-calc(100% /3) -moz-calc(100% /3);
    -ms-grid-columns: calc(100% /3) calc(100% /3) calc(100% /3);
    grid-template-columns: -webkit-calc(100% /3) -webkit-calc(100% /3) -webkit-calc(100% /3);
    grid-template-columns: -moz-calc(100% /3) -moz-calc(100% /3) -moz-calc(100% /3);
    grid-template-columns: calc(100% /3) calc(100% /3) calc(100% /3);
    list-style: none;
}

.interview-container > .on-call > ul > li {
    	grid-template-areas:
    	"name"
    	"title";
    padding: 0 1em;
    text-align: center;
    position: relative;
}

.interview-container > .on-call > ul > li > span.doctor_name {
    --wght: 900;
    display: -webkit-box;
    display: -webkit-flex;
    display: -moz-box;
    display: -ms-flexbox;
    display: flex;
    grid-area: "name";
    -webkit-box-pack: center;
    -webkit-justify-content: center;
       -moz-box-pack: center;
    	-ms-flex-pack: center;
    		justify-content: center;
    font-weight: 900;
    color: #fcfcfc;
    color: rgb(var(--swatch-menutxt-light-color));
    margin-top: 1em;
    padding: 0.5em 0;
    font-family: var(--title-font);
}

.interview-container > .on-call > ul > li:nth-of-type(1) > span.doctor_name {
    background-color: rgba(var(--jates-color));
}

.interview-container > .on-call > ul > li:nth-of-type(2) > span.doctor_name {
    background-color: rgba(var(--harmon-color));
}

.interview-container > .on-call > ul > li:nth-of-type(3) > span.doctor_name {
    background-color: rgba(var(--fitzwell-color));
}

.interview-container > .on-call > ul > li > span.doctor_title {
    display: -webkit-box;
    display: -webkit-flex;
    display: -moz-box;
    display: -ms-flexbox;
    display: flex;
    grid-area: "title";
    -webkit-box-pack: center;
    -webkit-justify-content: center;
       -moz-box-pack: center;
    	-ms-flex-pack: center;
    		justify-content: center;
    line-height: 1.2;
    margin-top: 0.5em;
    font-size: 1.1em;
    font-family: var(--title-font);
}

.interview-container > .interview-main {
    background-color: rgb(var(--very-light-gray-monochrome), 1);
    padding: 1rem 0.5em 0em 0.5em;
    margin-top: 1rem;
    border: 0.0625rem #CCCCCC solid;
    border: 0.0625rem rgb(var(--swatch-border-color)) solid;
    position: relative;
}

.interview-container > .interview-main > br {
    display: none;
}

.interview-container > .interview-main > div {
    display: block;
    width: -webkit-calc(100% - 3rem);
    width: -moz-calc(100% - 3rem);
    width: calc(100% - 3rem);
    background-color: rgb(var(--very-light-gray-monochrome), 1);
    padding: 0.75rem 0.75rem 0.5rem;
    margin: 1.5rem 0.5rem 0.5rem 0.5rem;
    border: 0.25rem rgb(var(--swatch-border-color)) solid;
    position: relative;
    font-size: 1rem;
    line-height: 1.5;
    overflow-wrap: break-word;
    word-wrap: break-word;
    -webkit-hyphens: auto;
    -ms-hyphens: auto;
    -moz-hyphens: auto;
    hyphens: auto;
}

.interview-container > .interview-main > div > p:first-of-type {
    margin-top: 0;
}

.interview-container > .interview-main > div > p:last-of-type {
    margin-bottom: 0;
}

.interview-container > .interview-main > div::before {
    --wght: 600;
    content: " ";
    position: absolute;
    top: -1.25rem;
    left: -0.5rem;
    padding: 0.5em 0.5em;
    color: rgb(var(--white-monochrome));
    font-weight: 600;
    letter-spacing: 0.05em;
    text-indent: 0;
    display: -webkit-box;
    display: -webkit-flex;
    display: -moz-box;
    display: -ms-flexbox;
    display: flex;
    line-height: 1;
    white-space: nowrap;
}

.interview-container > .interview-main > div.jates {
    background-color: rgba(var(--jates-color), 0.1);
    border-color: rgba(var(--jates-color));
}

.interview-container > .interview-main > div.harmon {
    background-color: rgba(var(--harmon-color), 0.1);
    border-color: rgba(var(--harmon-color));
}

.interview-container > .interview-main > div.fitzwell {
    background-color: rgba(var(--fitzwell-color), 0.1);
    border-color: rgba(var(--fitzwell-color));
}

.interview-container > .interview-main > div.jates::before {
    content: "Dr. Jates";
    background-color: rgba(var(--jates-color));
}

.interview-container > .interview-main > div.harmon::before {
    content: "Dr. Harmon";
    background-color: rgba(var(--harmon-color));
}

.interview-container > .interview-main > div.fitzwell::before {
    content: "Dr. Fitzwell";
    background-color: rgba(var(--fitzwell-color));
}

/* Footnotes */
.footnote_block {
    --wght: 600;
    all: initial;
    display: -webkit-inline-box;
    display: -webkit-inline-flex;
    display: -moz-inline-box;
    display: -ms-inline-flexbox;
    display: inline-flex;
    font-family: var(--body-font);
    font-weight: 600;
    font-size: 0.8em;
    margin-left: 0;
    margin-right: -0.25em;
}

.footnote_block .footnote_content,
.footnote_block .footnote_content * {
    --wght: 600;
    all: initial;
    display: block;
    position: absolute;
    right: -12rem;
    width: 8rem;
    line-height: 1.2;
    border: 0.125rem solid rgb(var(--black-monochrome));
    font-family: var(--body-font);
    font-weight: 600;
    padding: 1rem;
    text-shadow: initial;
    -webkit-filter: initial;
    filter: initial;
    color: rgb(var(--black-monochrome));
    -webkit-transition:
    	opacity 600ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    	-webkit-transform 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    transition:
    	opacity 600ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    	-webkit-transform 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    -o-transition:
    	opacity 600ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    	-o-transform 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    -moz-transition:
    	opacity 600ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    	transform 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    	-moz-transform 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    transition:
    	opacity 600ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    	transform 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    transition:
    	opacity 600ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    	transform 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    	-webkit-transform 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    	-moz-transform 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    	-o-transform 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    pointer-events: none
    z-index: 20;
}

.footnote_block .footnote_num {
    --wght: 900;
    text-shadow: initial;
    font-weight: 900;
    color: rgb(var(--white-monochrome));
    cursor: pointer;
    -webkit-box-shadow: initial;
    -moz-box-shadow: initial;
    box-shadow: initial;

    width: 1em;

    display: -moz-box;

    align-items: center;
    -webkit-box-pack: center;
    -webkit-justify-content: center;
    -moz-box-pack: center;
    -ms-flex-pack: center;
    justify-content: center;
    z-index: 1;
}

.footnote_block .footnote_content::before {
    --wght: 900;
    content: " ";
    position: absolute;
    font-size: 1em;
    top: -0.5em;
    left: -0.5em;
    text-shadow: initial;
    font-weight: 900;

    cursor: pointer;

    -moz-box-shadow: initial;
    box-shadow: initial;
    -webkit-filter: unset;
    filter: unset;
    -webkit-transform: unset;
    -moz-transform: unset;
    -ms-transform: unset;
    -o-transform: unset;
    transform: unset;
    line-height: 2;
    background-color: rgb(var(--link-color));

    width: 1em;

}

.footnote_block.seven .footnote_num + div {
    -webkit-transform: translateX(-25%);
    -moz-transform: translateX(-25%);
    -ms-transform: translateX(-25%);
    -o-transform: translateX(-25%);
    transform: translateX(-25%);
}

.footnote_block.seven .footnote_num:hover + div {

    -moz-transform: translateX(1rem);
    -ms-transform: translateX(1rem);
    -o-transform: translateX(1rem);
    transform: translateX(1rem);
}

.footnote_block.eight .footnote_num + div {
    -webkit-transform: translateX(-webkit-calc(275% + 1rem));
    -moz-transform: translateX(-moz-calc(275% + 1rem));
    -ms-transform: translateX(calc(275% + 1rem));
    -o-transform: translateX(calc(275% + 1rem));
    transform: translateX(calc(275% + 1rem));
}

.footnote_block.eight .footnote_num:hover + div {
    -webkit-transform: translateX(-webkit-calc(300% + 2rem));
    -moz-transform: translateX(-moz-calc(300% + 2rem));
    -ms-transform: translateX(calc(300% + 2rem));
    -o-transform: translateX(calc(300% + 2rem));
    transform: translateX(calc(300% + 2rem));
}

.footnote_block .footnote_num + div {
    opacity: 0;
}

.footnote_block .footnote_num:hover + div {
    opacity: 1;
}


.footnote_block.seven .footnote_content::before {
    content: "7";
}

.footnote_block.eight .footnote_content::before {
    content: "8";
}

[[/module]]
[[div_ class="page-title"]]
SCP-4485 ORIENTATION PART 2 OF 8
[[/div]]
@@ @@
[[div_ class="warning-container"]]
[[div_ class="warning-top"]]
!! REMOTE TERMINAL MESSAGE !!
[[/div]]
[[div class="warning-body"]]
Your progression is most satisfactory. Dr. Lark has joined me and requested to observe your progress in person. However, his request was denied. It is imperative that you complete the orientation in isolation until an acceptable level of comprehension is observed.

I already anticipate you have many questions. However, all out-going communication is forbidden. As such, I can give you answers to a few common inquiries that other employees have had:

# **What level of comprehension is deemed acceptable?:** This is on a supervisor to supervisor basis. The Foundation has requested that we accept nothing but a perfect level of understanding. However, I tend to only require this if the Researcher will be directly involved in SCP-4485. As you will be directly involved, I will accept nothing but perfection.
# **What will happen if I am found to fail the orientation?:** You will be terminated.
# **What does "comprehension" mean?:** A depressingly common question. It means you must understand the risk and logical danger that SCP-4485 poses. If you need to ask this question, you likely will not pass.

That is all. I will be stepping away from the Terminal for a time and Dr. Lark will observe in my absence.

Please Continue.

Lloyd Michaelson
Director,  Site-12
[[/div]]
[[/div]]

------

[[table class="wiki-content-table"]]
[[row]]
[[hcell colspan="2"]]
SCP-4485 DOCUMENT THREE
[[/hcell]]
[[/row]]
[[row]]
[[cell]]
[[span]]GROUP OF INTEREST[[/span]]
[[span]]DATABASE ENTRY[[/span]]
[[span]]"HANSARP"[[/span]]
[[/cell]]
[[cell]]
[[span]]Originally filed:[[/span]]
[[span]]Saturday, 29 July, 1967[[/span]]
[[span]]Archived pulled: [[/span]]
[[html]]
<head>
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css?family=PT+Mono">
<style type="text/css">
body {
    --wght: 900;
    color: rgb(252, 252, 252);
    font-family: "PT Mono", "Courier New", Courier, monospace;
    font-size: 1.055rem;
    font-weight: 900;
    line-height: 1.406rem;
    -webkit-font-kerning: normal;
    font-kerning: normal;
    font-variant-numeric: normal;
    -webkit-font-feature-settings: "lnum", "kern", "liga", "clig", "calt";
    -moz-font-feature-settings: "lnum", "kern", "liga", "clig", "calt";
    font-feature-settings: "lnum", "kern", "liga", "clig", "calt";
    text-rendering: auto;
    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
    -moz-osx-font-smoothing: grayscale;
    orphans: 2;
    -webkit-hyphens: manual;
    -moz-hyphens: manual;
    -ms-hyphens: manual;
    hyphens: manual;
    word-break: normal;
    text-transform: uppercase;
}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<center>
<script>

    var mydate=new Date()
    var year=mydate.getYear()
    
    if (year < 1000)
    	year+=1900
    
    var day=mydate.getDay()
    var month=mydate.getMonth()
    var daym=mydate.getDate()
    
    if (daym<10)
    	daym="0"+daym
    	
    var dayarray=new Array("Sunday","Monday","Tuesday","Wednesday","Thursday",
    						"Friday","Saturday")
    var montharray=new Array("January","February","March","April","May","June",
    						"July","August","September","October","November","December")
    
    document.write("",dayarray[day]+", "+daym+" "+montharray[month]+", "+year, "")

</script>
</center>
</body>
[[/html]] 
[[/cell]]
[[/row]]
[[/table]]
[[collapsible show="⇛ CLICK TO BEGIN ⇚" hide="⤋ HIDE DOCUMENT ⤋"]]
    [[div_ class="database_container"]]
    	[[div_ class="database_main"]]
    		[[div_ class="database_title"]]
    		Group of Interest Profile ◦ Site-35 Department of External Groups
    		[[/div]]
    		[[div_ class="database_table top"]]									
    			[[div_ class="cell" data-label="Group Name"]]
    			"HANSARP"
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div_ class="cell" data-label="Aliases"]]
    			Unknown
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div_ class="cell" data-label="Leadership"]]
    			Unknown
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div_ class="hansarp_logo"]]
    				[[div_ class="cell"]]
[[image https://scp-wiki.wikidot.com/local--files/fragment:scp-4485-2/hansarplogo.png]]
    				[[/div]]
    				[[div_ class="cell"]]
    				ONLY KNOWN "HANSARP" LOGOTYPE
    				[[/div]]
    			[[/div]]
    		[[/div]]
    		[[div_ class="database_table main"]]
    			[[div_ class="locations"]]
    					[[div_ class="cell" data-label="Primary Areas of Activity"]]
[[ul]]
[[li]]Southern Germany[[/li]]
[[li]]Austria[[/li]]
[[li]]Switzerland[[/li]]
[[li]]Hungary[[/li]]
[[li]]Yugoslavia[[/li]]
[[li]]Czechoslovakia[[/li]]
[[/ul]]
    					[[/div]]
[[div_ class="cell" data-label="Additional Areas of Activity"]]
[[ul]]
[[li]]SE France[[/li]]
[[li]]Northern Italy[[/li]]
[[li]]Switzerland[[/li]]
[[li]]NE Spain[[/li]]
[[li]]Denmark[[/li]]
[[li]]Western Soviet Socialist Republic[[/li]]
[[/ul]]
    					[[/div]]
    					[[div_ class="cell" data-label="Limited Activity"]]
[[ul]]
[[li]]Greece[[/li]]
[[li]]Belgium[[/li]]
[[li]]United Kingdom[[/li]]
[[li]]Egypt[[/li]]
[[/ul]]
    					[[/div]]
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div_ class="main_content"]]
    				[[div_ class="cell" data-label="Group Activity Pattern Analysis"]]
    					[[div]]
    Group activity centers around the creation of anomalous art pieces and the creation of anomalous machines. While the style and structure of these pieces differs, all appear to serve the same purpose of altering reality.

    What is most unusual, however, is the transient nature of these pieces and machines. In the case of nearly all recovered artifacts, the pieces have been placed in locations where they would be visible to a large number of civilians (typically at intersections, within shopping centers, on the sides of buildings, etc.) but do not remain long after they are noticed. In many cases, the pieces have self-removed by the time Foundation assets have moved to intercept them. To date, all pieces have been tagged with the word "HANSARP" in orange spray paint, and signed with a crude drawing of a vertical fish.
    					[[/div]]
    				[[/div]]
    				[[div_ class="cell"  data-label="Group Description"]]
    					[[div]]
    It is currently believed that "HANSARP" is a cultural evolution	within a south German "Are We Cool Yet?" sect, designed to disrupt the tenuous peace within the civilian population in the area. Like many AWCY? sects, "HANSARP" creates pieces of obscene or provocative "art", which are then placed in areas where they would do the most harm and left to disturb population centers. The primary differentiation between the two groups, however, is that while artists within AWCY? sects tend to seek attention personally through the use of pseudonyms, "HANSARP" members do not identify themselves by any name other than "HANSARP".

    Documents recovered from a coordinated information exchange with the Global Occult Coalition European Taskforce indicate that "HANSARP" has additional ties to high-ranking members of the Church of the Broken God. These connections are likely the source of the anomalous machinery often discovered at "HANSARP" activity sites, though their relation to the Church of the Broken God is still unknown. Analysis of these machines prior to their self-removal has resulted in no definitive answer to their purpose; the machines simply perform a function for a short period of time, and then disassemble afterwards. While it is assumed that they, like the anomalous art pieces attributed to "HANSARP", exist only to disrupt the public, this assumption is not conclusive.
    					[[/div]]
    				[[/div]]
    			[[/div]]
    		[[/div]]
    	[[/div]]
    [[/div]]
[[/collapsible]]

------
