[[div_ class="responsive_table"]]
    [[div_ class="table_row"]]
    	[[div_ class="table_data" data-label="ID#"]]
    	4485/35B
    	[[/div]]
    	[[div_ class="table_data" data-label="DISCOVERY DATE"]]
    	3 March, 1967
    	[[/div]]
    	[[div_ class="table_data" data-label="TITLE"]]
    	"Dog Will Sniff" by HANSARP
    	[[/div]]
    	[[div_ class="table_data" data-label="LOCATION OF DISCOVERY"]]
    	Restaurant in Vienna, Austria
    	[[/div]]
    	[[div_ class="table_data" data-label="SUBJECT & EFFECTS"]]
    	Watercolor painting of an artist painting a watercolor painting. The artist's head is located on its back, facing down, and its features are indistinguishable. Exposed subjects reported seeing duplicates of themselves, and duplicates of the duplicates within the restaurant.
    	[[/div]]
    	[[div_ class="table_data" data-label="CURRENT LOCATION"]]
    	N/A. Painting was reduced to colored water after two hours.
    	[[/div]]
    [[/div]]
    [[div_ class="table_row"]]
    	[[div_ class="table_data" data-label="ID#"]]
    	4485/35G
    	[[/div]]
    	[[div_ class="table_data" data-label="DISCOVERY DATE"]]
    	23 May, 1967
    	[[/div]]
    	[[div_ class="table_data" data-label="TITLE"]]
    	"I looked up on upon Babylon ylon" by HANSARP
    	[[/div]]
    	[[div_ class="table_data" data-label="LOCATION OF DISCOVERY"]]
    	Judicial building in Munich, West Germany
    	[[/div]]
    	[[div_ class="table_data" data-label="SUBJECT & EFFECTS"]]
    	A rough pencil sketch of three children consuming each others' feet. Two of the children appear to be weeping, one is laughing. Individuals exposed to the piece began to suffer from the inability to pass through doorways, and were unable to escape the building for some time. This continued until the piece self-removed from the first floor lobby wall.
    	[[/div]]
    	[[div_ class="table_data" data-label="CURRENT LOCATION"]]
    	N/A. The piece is reported to have begun curling up on itself after several hours, and continued to roll tighter until it no longer existed.
    	[[/div]]
    [[/div]]
    [[div_ class="table_row"]]
    	[[div_ class="table_data" data-label="ID#"]]
    	4485/36K
    	[[/div]]
    	[[div_ class="table_data" data-label="DISCOVERY DATE"]]
    	19 October, 1969
    	[[/div]]
    	[[div_ class="table_data" data-label="TITLE"]]
    	"Untitled '95" by HANSARP
    	[[/div]]
    	[[div_ class="table_data" data-label="LOCATION OF DISCOVERY"]]
    	In a cellar beneath a home in Frankfurt, West Germany
    	[[/div]]
    	[[div_ class="table_data" data-label="SUBJECT & EFFECTS"]]
    	A small, vinyl-covered clockwork machine that produces a small, two dimensional paper fish once every sixteen minutes. Each fish contains the text of a single racial epithet, though these words only appear offensive or obscene to individuals of a different ethnic background, and appear to be the words "what will you do?" to those of the intended background.
    	[[/div]]
    	[[div_ class="table_data" data-label="CURRENT LOCATION"]]
    	Currently in a storage locker at Site-35. Ceased functioning after it was removed from the house. Believed to have generated over 1,900 pieces of paper fish prior to its discovery.
    	[[/div]]
    [[/div]]
    [[div_ class="table_row"]]
    	[[div_ class="table_data" data-label="ID#"]]
    	4485/35AA
    	[[/div]]
    	[[div_ class="table_data" data-label="DISCOVERY DATE"]]
    	21 October, 1969
    	[[/div]]
    	[[div_ class="table_data" data-label="TITLE"]]
    	"dak dak dit va va ti da da" by HANSARP
    	[[/div]]
    	[[div_ class="table_data" data-label="LOCATION OF DISCOVERY"]]
    	A public square in St. Gallen, Switzerland
    	[[/div]]
    	[[div_ class="table_data" data-label="SUBJECT & EFFECTS"]]
    	A green arrow painted on a lamp post pointed upwards. Individuals exposed to this piece directly begin to feel severe anxiety and self-loathing. Spreads to individuals who interact with these individuals
    		[[div_ class="footnote_block seven"]]
    			[[div_ class="footnote_num"]]
    								7  
    						[[/div]]
    			[[div_ class="footnote_content"]]
    				This effect is poorly understood, and is still under investigation.
    			[[/div]]
    					[[/div]]
    	.
    	[[/div]]
    	[[div_ class="table_data" data-label="CURRENT LOCATION"]]
    	Paint could not be removed through traditional means. Light post was removed and sandblasted into metal shavings. The metal shavings retained the same characteristics of the light post, and were moved to a secure vault.
    	[[/div]]
    [[/div]]
    [[div_ class="table_row"]]

    	4485/35AV

[[table class="wiki-content-table"]]
[[row]]
[[hcell colspan="2"]]
SCP-4485 DOCUMENT FIVE
[[/hcell]]
[[/row]]
[[row]]
[[cell]]
[[span]]INTERNAL INTERVIEW[[/span]]
[[/cell]]
[[cell]]
[[span]]CONDUCTED 10 MAY, 1983[[/span]]
[[/cell]]
[[/row]]
[[/table]]
[[collapsible show="⇛ CLICK TO BEGIN ⇚" hide="⤋ HIDE DOCUMENT ⤋"]]
@@ @@
    [[div_ class="interview-foreword blockquote"]]
    	Below is a transcript of an interview via three-way telephone call following the appearance of Artifact 4485/47X conducted by Dr. Viktor Jates with Dr. Isaac Harmon and Dr. Leonard Fitzwell.
    [[/div]]
    [[div_ class="interview-container"]]
    	[[div_ class="on-call"]]
    		[[ul]]
    			[[li]][[span class="doctor_name"]]Dr. Viktor Jates[[/span]][[span class="doctor_title"]]Foundation European Mathematical Studies Director[[/span]][[/li]]
    			[[li]][[span class="doctor_name"]]Dr. Isaac Harmon[[/span]][[span class="doctor_title"]]Foundation European Department of Containment Director[[/span]][[/li]]
    			[[li]][[span class="doctor_name"]]Dr. Leonard Fitzwell[[/span]][[span class="doctor_title"]]European GOI Liaison[[/span]][[/li]]
    		[[/ul]]
    	[[/div]]
    	[[div_ class="interview-main"]]
    					[[div class="jates"]]
    			Dr. Harmon, hello. Dr. Fitzwell, thank you both for taking my call.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="harmon"]]
    			Of course.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="fitzwell"]]
    			Isaac has seen our report?
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="harmon"]]
    			On the ride over, but not in depth.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="jates"]]
    			I was hoping you could, uh, illuminate him, on-
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="harmon"]]
    			I'm just trying to wrap my head around what you're saying here, Fitz. About this whole art thing, it sounds like you're saying nothing we've done here has accomplished anything.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="fitzwell"]]
    			Well that's just it, Isaac. We've missed the forest for the trees on this one, and I think we're in dire trouble.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="harmon"]]
    			Explain.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="fitzwell"]]
    			Do you know when the last time we were contacted by an outside group to investigate itself?
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="harmon"]]
    			I- what? That's never happened.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="jates"]]
    			As of Tuesday, it has.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="harmon"]]
    			What?
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="fitzwell"]]
    			The largest anom-art community in Europe is "Are We Cool Yet?" They're fairly fragmented, but bound by this sort of loose association with an individual called "The Critic". 
    			
    			That individual contacted one of our field agents on Tuesday to request that we look into the well-being of one of their studios near Munich.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="harmon"]]
    			I don't understand. Why would they try to contact us about that?
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="jates"]]
    			That was our question. The Critic claims that the studio in question, some hundred or so individuals, has just dropped off the face of the earth. Local authorities weren't able to help, and they were worried that contacting the Coalition would result in more deaths than recoveries. 
    			
    			So they contacted us instead.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="harmon"]]
    			And? What did we find?
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="fitzwell"]]
    			Nothing, Isaac. We raided the studio and found //nothing//. Not that we didn't find signs of habitation-
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="jates"]]
    			Oh yes, we found //plenty// of that.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="fitzwell"]]
    			-it's that by all accounts the people who had been living there just got up one day and left.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="harmon"]]
    			Do we think this is some kind of anomalous influence, or something mundane? Maybe they ran out of money, or-
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="jates"]]
    			No, they wouldn't have just left all of their stuff there. Their equipment, belongings, it was all left untouched.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="harmon"]]
    			So what does this have to do with HANSARP?
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="fitzwell"]]
    			When our field agent reported back to The Critic what we had found, he revealed some things about HANSARP we didn't know. He thinks HANSARP has been influencing a group of disillusioned members of Are We Cool Yet? over the last several months, and that influence might have resulted in their disappearance.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="harmon"]]
    			Influencing how?
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="jates"]]
    			There's a growing sentiment within that community that the effort to produce creatively is not worth the outcome, because they are no closer to self-actualization. 
    			
    			They call it wanting to become "cool", but it's just a desire to reach some state of synthesis with themselves and their artform. Their inability to unify the "self" and the "cool" is what we typically see as a motivating factor for the group.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="fitzwell"]]
    			The Critic poses that the most vocal voices of displeasure have come out of south Germany, where more and more of them are turning to violence and sadism in attempting to reach this state of "cool". The Critic says he's been able to moderate most of the more radical influences, but not HANSARP. 
    			
    			Something about HANSARP has really set these people off, and then they disappear suddenly.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="harmon"]]
    			I still- I don't understand this, Leonard. This HANSARP and Are We Cool Yet?, our documentation says that they're the same group, but you're suggesting they're separate? Where are you getting this information from?
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="fitzwell"]]
    			Isaac, they //have// to be separate. 
    			
    			Aside from creating anomalous art pieces, they're totally different. Are We Cool Yet? seeks to make art pieces that establish the creator as an elevated member within the group - something closer to "cool" than others. 
    			
    			HANSARP doesn't care about creators, or about the art work at all. HANSARP's pieces aren't trying to create status or position, they're nothing. They don't mean anything. There's no linearity between them, and yet they continue to be created. More people are disappearing, specifically those who encountered these pieces. 
    			
    			That chapter of Are We Cool Yet? They were exposed to a piece the week before, hung right outside their studio. Now they're gone.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="harmon"]]
    			Alright, so... so what does this all mean? What does this mean for those kids who disappeared in Germany?
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="jates"]]
    			We, well...
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="fitzwell"]]
    			We're beginning to think that they //are// HANSARP.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="harmon"]]
    			What?
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="fitzwell"]]
    			They didn't used to be, but they are now. 
    			
    			HANSARP isn't like other outside groups, where you're recruited or sign up or just find yourself within their ranks. HANSARP is like... like a disease. Like a virus. You see it and it works its way into your soul and by the time you realize what has happened to you, you're HANSARP. 
    			
    			Which leads to our next point...
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="jates"]]
    			We don't think the pieces of art are anomalous.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="fitzwell"]]
    			What do you think about sideways eyes on █████ ██████ ██ ████?
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="harmon"]]
    			Excuse me?
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="jates"]]
    			(//Interrupting//) Not in, not in the way we had originally believed, anyway. 
    			
    			Something else you need to understand - HANSARP is a dadaist movement. 
    			
    			Within dada, there is no meaning - reason and logic are considered detrimental to the human condition in favor of disorder. They don't have a set of beliefs that you could call consistent because they have given up on consistency.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="fitzwell"]]
    			It's not shocking that they would target groups who are at a growing risk of frustration due to the impossibility of their goals, because HANSARP's philosophy precludes the possibility of "goals" as a concept. You can't have "goals" when there is nothing to be gained. 
    			
    			But then by the same token, what is "cool" if there are no inherent rules governing what "cool" is? If there are no ideals or definite purpose, then who's to say that everything isn't cool?
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="harmon"]]
    			So what... what does any of this have to do with the art pieces being anomalous?
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div]]
    			//Silence.//
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="harmon"]]
    			Viktor?
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="jates"]]
    			The pieces are anomalous, but not... not in the way we thought they were. 
    			
    			The book is the same way. 
    			
    			We thought the book was changing reality, but it's- the book hasn't done anything since we put it in containment. 
    			
    			The art pieces don't //do// anything when we put them in containment. We only see the sort of anomalous activity we associate with the book when other people are involved. People who see these art pieces, or who read SCP-4485, they're exposed to something that makes them question fundamental truths about their lives. It happened here, at Site-35.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="harmon"]]
    			What? What happened?
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="jates"]]
    			We had a group of our own researchers studying SCP-4485, reading it and trying to figure out some sort of pattern in the madness of it.
    			
    			We eventually started to notice that some of them were growing... detached. 
    			
    			They stopped interacting with others around them, started to ask strange questions. Some of them started to become agitated, angry. They'd rant and rave about some- some ideology, or some position, something political. 
    			
    			Our staff psychologists spoke to them, and they looked at their background profiles. The ones who were becoming violent were those with a history of- of strong positions on certain issues, things they'd spoken up about in the past: political movements, workplace issues, that sort of thing. 
    			
    			The rest just- just hadn't. But they were all being changed, like HANSARP was crystallizing their apathy, or their zealotry. When they started to change, the site started to change.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="harmon"]]
    			How?
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="jates"]]
    			People began to see things that weren't there. Doors that didn't exist, impossible changes in geometry. HANSARP took credit for this in a local paper, but there had been no contact with these people and HANSARP, except by reading the book.
    			
    			Over time, the same thing happened to the psychologists. Talking to these people changes the way you see the world, and once you've become like they are, the world changes to fit what you see. Madness.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="harmon"]]
    			Where are these researchers now?
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="fitzwell"]]
    			Isolated. At different locked safehouses. 
    			
    			We can't change them, and they're still- they're changing. Keeping them apart helps mitigate some of the issues, but we keep finding more every day.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="jates"]]
    			We're starting to see this everywhere, Isaac. All over Europe. 
    			
    			Any site that has ever housed a HANSARP artifact, or SCP-4485, or even... even someone who had read SCP-4485, or- or...
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="harmon"]]
    			You cannot be serious...
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="fitzwell"]]
    			Isaac, they're everywhere. We have affected researchers everywhere.
    			
    			And it doesn't take much - they'll ask an innocuous question, maybe questioning a strongly held position. But something about HANSARP makes those questions stick, and then... then they become carriers for it. They become HANSARP.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="harmon"]]
    			This... the staff rotations... all of Europe, all of our sites...
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="jates"]]
    			The only people who weren't affected were people on my team. People who were trained to use formula and theory to discern truth. They saw the same things other people did, but they didn't succumb to it. They weren't changed.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="harmon"]]
    			We- we need to begin training, we need to get these individuals at- at every site, every site could be affected.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="jates"]]
    			There aren't enough, Isaac. We would need an army of mathematicians, and that just- it doesn't exist.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="harmon"]]
    			I- (//pauses//) -alright. 
    			
    			I will discuss this with my team. We will work on taking the necessary steps to mitigate this issue. 
    			
    			(//Pauses//) 
    			
    			Leonard, we'll need to get you evacuated from Site-35; if what Viktor is saying is true, and you haven't had this sort of intensive training, then-
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="fitzwell"]]
    			No, Isaac. 
    			
    			There is work that needs to be done here. We can still communicate with those affected. Another group went missing recently, Broken God faithful each, and the connection between the two is undeniable. We... I will know when it is too late for us to accomplish anything here, Isaac. Then I will leave.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="harmon"]]
    			Please do. We need you, Leonard. Now more than ever. 
    			
    			I'm going to report this to Director Rains and Director Quin. You are right. We have work to do. Be safe.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="fitzwell"]]
    			You as well, Isaac.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div]]
    			//Dr. Harmon disconnects.//
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="jates"]]
    			How much longer do you have, Leonard?
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="fitzwell"]]
    			(//Laughs//) 
    			
    			Truthfully, I don't even know if you are real, Viktor. 
    			
    			Everything feels the same. This conversation feels the same. I will make do until I cannot make do any longer, and then... well.
    			
    			Hopefully Isaac comes up with something before then.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="jates"]]
    			The Broken God link, what do you see there?
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="fitzwell"]]
    			That's who is building these machines, of course. 
    			
    			I think we're- the more I look into this crawling madness I know follows me wherever I go, the more I see the method. A god that cannot be rebuilt. 
    			
    			A state of cool that cannot be reached. 
    			
    			If you break down the structures that determine what "broken" is, or what "cool" is, what are you left with? 
    			
    			(//Pauses//) 
    			
    			We should've known sooner.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="jates"]]
    			We couldn't have known.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="fitzwell"]]
    			We could have tried. 
    			
    			I kept that book in my study for a month, you know? 
    			
    			Just reading it. 
    			
    			Trying to see what in it could compel a man to remove his own face, just in order to see... to see something. We should have rules for that sort of thing.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="jates"]]
    			I know.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="fitzwell"]]
    			Viktor, I- have I said anything to you yet? Anything that you- that you could see didn't make sense? Something out of place? I think, I've tried to think and see, if there would be anything. 
    			
    			Tell me truthfully, Viktor. Please.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="jates"]]
    			Just once. Sideways eyes.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="fitzwell"]]
    			Ah. On shores of glass ████ ████ ████ █ ██████ ██ █████. I remember. (//Pauses//) Just now?
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="jates"]]
    			Yes.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="fitzwell"]]
    			Isaac?
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="jates"]]
    			We know. We'll take care of him.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="fitzwell"]]
    			Very good, very... very good. Thank you, Viktor. Your team does extraordinary... extraordinary work.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="jates"]]
    			Thank you, Leonard.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="fitzwell"]]
    			(//Pauses//) I'm sorry, Viktor. I'm sorry.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="jates"]]
    			It's alright, Leonard. It will be alright.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div class="fitzwell"]]
    			I'm sorry, I'm- goodnight, Viktor.
    			[[/div]]
    			[[div]]
    			//Dr. Fitzwell disconnects. Call ends.//
    			[[/div]]
    		[[/div]]
    	[[/div]]
    [[/collapsible]]

------

[[div_ class="warning-container"]]
[[html]]
<!DOCTYPE html>

<head>
    <base target="_parent">
    <meta charset="utf-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
    <meta http-equiv="X-UA-Compatible" content="IE=edge">
    <link rel="stylesheet" type="text/css" href="https://d3g0gp89917ko0.cloudfront.net/v--b23e476b7ade/common--theme/base/css/style.css">
    <link rel="stylesheet" type="text/css" href="https://nu-scptheme.github.io/Black-Highlighter/css/min/black-highlighter.min.css">
    <link rel="stylesheet" type="text/css" href="https://scp-wiki.wdfiles.com/local--code/theme%3Athe-foundation-theme/1">
    <style>
    	html#html-block-html, 
    	html#html-block-html body {
    		display: flex;
    		align-items: center;
    		justify-content: center;
    		height: 100% !important;
    		width: 100% !important;
    	}
    	.continue-button {
    		--wght: 900;
    		border: 0.313rem solid transparent;
    		background: rgba(var(--swatch-primary-darkest));
    		color: rgba(var(--white-monochrome));
    		padding: 0.95em 1em;
    		width: 15em;
    		max-height: 4em;
    		transition:
    			text-indent 1.2s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			width 1.2s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			max-height 1.2s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			color 1.2s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			background 1.2s 1.3s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			transform 0.2s 1.3s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			box-shadow 0.2s 1.3s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    		white-space: nowrap;
    		font-weight: 900;
    		margin: 1rem auto;
    		font-family: var(--title-font);
    		line-height: 1;
    		font-size: 1.5em;
    		text-align: center;
    		display: flex;
    		flex-wrap: wrap;
    		flex-direction: column;
    		align-items: center;
    		justify-content: center;
    		position: relative;
    		text-indent: 0;
    	}

    	.continue-button a {
    		position: absolute;
    		top: 0;
    		left: 0;
    		width: 100%;
    		height: 100%;
    		z-index: 5;
    		cursor: progress;
    	}

    	.continue-button a.active-link {
    		cursor: pointer !important;
    	}

    	.continue-button br {
    		display: none;
    	}

    	.continue-button::before {
    		pointer-events: none;
    		content: " ";
    		position: absolute;
    		top: -0.625rem;
    		left: -0.625rem;
    		width: calc(100% + 1.25rem);
    		height: calc(100% + 1.25rem);
    		background-color: rgb(var(--swatch-primary));
    		background-image: url("https://scp-wiki.wikidot.com/local--files/theme:the-foundation-theme/foundation-circles-bg.png");
    		background-size: 50%;
    		background-attachment: fixed;
    		opacity: 0;
    		transition:
    			opacity 1.2s 1.3s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			background-size 1.2s 1.3s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    		background-position: center center;
    		z-index: -1;
    	}

    	.continue-button:hover::before {
    		opacity: 1;
    		background-size: 100%;
    	}

    	.continue-button .supervisor {
    		max-height: 0;
    		font-size: 0.85em;
    		text-align: center;
    		display: flex;
    		align-items: center;
    		justify-content: center;
    		opacity: 0;
    		transition:
    			opacity 1.1s 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			max-height 1.1s 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    	}

    	.continue-button:hover .supervisor {
    		opacity: 1;
    		max-height: 2em;
    		transition:
    			opacity 1.1s 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			max-height 1.1s 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    	}

    	.continue-button span {
    		--wght: 400;
    		display: inline-flex;
    		transform: translateX(13rem) translateY(0.5rem);
    		font-weight: 400;
    		color: rgba(var(--white-monochrome), 0);
    		opacity: 0;
    		transition:
    			opacity 1.1s 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			transform 1.1s 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			color 1.4s 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			text-shadow 1.4s 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			font-size 0s 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    		position: absolute;
    		right: 1.875rem;
    		top: 0.367rem;
    		font-size: 0em;
    		border-radius: 50%;
    		background-color: darkgreen;
    		width: 3.750rem;
    		height: 3.750rem;
    		align-items: flex-start;
    		justify-content: flex-start;
    		text-shadow:
    			0 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1);
    		z-index: -1;
    		text-indent: 0;
    	}

    	.continue-button:hover {
    		text-indent: -4em;
    		background: rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome));
    		color: rgba(var(--white-monochrome));
    		width: 17em;
    		max-height: 10em;
    		transform: translateX(0.625rem) translateY(-0.625rem);
    		box-shadow:
    			0.625rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			-0.625rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0 0.625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0 -0.625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0.625rem 0.625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			-0.625rem 0.625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0.625rem -0.625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			-0.625rem -0.625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1);
    	}

    	.continue-button:hover span {
    		color: rgba(var(--white-monochrome), 1);
    		text-shadow:
    			0.0625rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			-0.0625rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0 0.0625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0 -0.0625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0.0625rem 0.0625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			-0.0625rem 0.0625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0.0625rem -0.0625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			-0.0625rem -0.0625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1);
    		transition:
    			opacity 1.1s 1.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			transform 1.4s 1.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			color 1.4s 6.8s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			text-shadow 1.4s 6.8s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			font-size 400ms 6.8s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    		transform: translateX(0) translateY(0.5rem);
    		opacity: 1;
    		font-size: 3em;
    	}

    	.continue-button span::before {
    		content: " ";
    		pointer-events: none;
    		position: absolute;
    		top: 0;
    		left: 0;
    		width: 100%;
    		height: 100%;
    		border-radius: 50%;
    		text-indent: -3em;
    		transform: rotateZ(360deg);
    	}

    	.continue-button:hover span::before {
    		transition:
    			transform 6.8s 1500ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    		animation:
    			border 5.4s cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.6, 0.8, 1) 1500ms 1 forwards,
    			rotate 1.125s linear 1500ms 5 backwards;
    		transform: rotateZ(0deg);
    	}

    	@keyframes border {
    		0% {
    			box-shadow:
    				inset 0 0.3125rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Top 1 */
    				inset 0.234375rem 0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Top Left 2 */
    				inset 0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Left 3 */
    				inset 0 -0.3125rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom 4*/
    				inset 0.234375rem -0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom Left 5 */
    				inset -0.234375rem -0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom Right 6 */
    				inset -0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Right 7 */
    				inset -0.234375rem 0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0);
    			/* Top Right 8 */
    		}

    		6.25% {
    			box-shadow:
    				inset 0 0.3125rem 0 rgba(var(--white-monochrome)),
    				/* Top 1 */
    				inset 0.234375rem 0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Top Left 2 */
    				inset 0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Left 3 */
    				inset 0 -0.3125rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom 4*/
    				inset 0.234375rem -0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom Left 5 */
    				inset -0.234375rem -0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom Right 6 */
    				inset -0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Right 7 */
    				inset -0.234375rem 0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0);
    			/* Top Right 8 */
    		}

    		93.75% {
    			box-shadow:
    				inset 0 0.3125rem 0 rgba(var(--white-monochrome)),
    				/* Top 1 */
    				inset 0.234375rem 0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Top Left 2 */
    				inset 0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Left 3 */
    				inset 0 -0.3125rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom 4*/
    				inset 0.234375rem -0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom Left 5 */
    				inset -0.234375rem -0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom Right 6 */
    				inset -0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Right 7 */
    				inset -0.234375rem 0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--white-monochrome));
    			/* Top Right 8 */
    		}

    		100% {
    			box-shadow:
    				inset 0 0.3125rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Top 1 */
    				inset 0.234375rem 0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Top Left 2 */
    				inset 0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Left 3 */
    				inset 0 -0.3125rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom 4*/
    				inset 0.234375rem -0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom Left 5 */
    				inset -0.234375rem -0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom Right 6 */
    				inset -0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Right 7 */
    				inset -0.234375rem 0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0);
    			/* Top Right 8 */

    		}
    	}

    	@keyframes rotate {
    		0% {
    			transform: rotate(0deg);
    		}

    		100% {
    			transform: rotate(360deg);
    		}
    	}
    	@media only screen and (max-width: 400px) {
    		.continue-button {
    			font-size: 1em;
    		}
    	}

    </style>
</head>

<body>
    <div id="continue-button-id" class="continue-button">
    	<a id="continue-link" href="https://scp-wiki.wikidot.com/scp-4485/offset/3"></a>
    	<div class="accept">CLICK TO SUBMIT APPROVAL</div>
    	<div class="supervisor">SUPERVISOR APPROVAL</div>
    	<br>
    	<span>✔</span>
    </div>
    <script>
    	var continueButton = document.getElementById("continue-button-id");  
    	var continueLink = document.getElementById("continue-link"); 
    	var to = null;     

    	function mouseOver() {
    		if(to){
    			clearTimeout(to);
    		}           
    		to = setTimeout(function(){                 
    			continueButton.classList.remove("active-hover");
    			continueLink.classList.add("active-link");                    
    		}, 5500);
    	}

    	function mouseOut() {
    		if(to){
    			clearTimeout(to);
    		}
    		continueLink.classList.remove("active-link");
    	}        

    	continueLink.addEventListener("mouseover", mouseOver);
    	continueLink.addEventListener("mouseout", mouseOut);
    </script>
</body>

[[/html]]
[[/div]]

	[[include theme:black-highlighter-theme]]
[[include theme:the-foundation-theme]]

[[module css]]
@import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css?family=Asset|Barrio|Eczar:600,700|Kumar+One|Major+Mono+Display|Miltonian+Tattoo|Ultra|Zilla+Slab+Highlight');

.warning-container iframe {
    min-height: 9.375rem;
}

.warning-container,
.warning-container * {
    -webkit-box-sizing: border-box;
    	 -moz-box-sizing: border-box;
    		box-sizing: border-box;
}

.warning-container {
    --wght: 900;
    width: 90%;
    margin: 0 auto;
    background-color: rgb(var(--dark-gray-monochrome));
    color: rgb(var(--swatch-text-light));
    font-weight: 900;
    border: 0.5em solid rgb(var(--swatch-primary-darkest));
}

.warning-container .warning-top {
    font-size: 200%;
    display: -webkit-box;
    display: -webkit-flex;
    display: -moz-box;
    display: -ms-flexbox;
    display: flex;
    -webkit-box-align: center;
    -webkit-align-items: center;
    -moz-box-align: center;
    -ms-flex-align: center;
    align-items: center;
    -webkit-box-pack: center;
    -webkit-justify-content: center;
    -moz-box-pack: center;
    -ms-flex-pack: center;
    justify-content: center;
    padding: 1rem;
    font-family: var(--title-font);
    background-color: rgb(var(--swatch-primary));
    background-image: url("https://scp-wiki.wikidot.com/local--files/theme:the-foundation-theme/foundation-circles-bg.png");
    -webkit-background-size: 50% 50%;
    -moz-background-size: 50%;
    -o-background-size: 50%;
    background-size: 50%;
    background-attachment: fixed;
    background-position: 50% 50%;
}

.warning-container .warning-body {
    --wght: 300;
    font-weight: 300;
    padding: 1rem;
    margin: 1rem;
    line-height: 1.5;
    font-size: 115%;
    font-family: var(--mono-font);
    background-color: rgb(var(--swatch-primary-darkest),0.25);
}

.warning-container .warning-body,
.warning-container .warning-body * {
    font-variation-settings: 
    	"wght" var(--mono-wght), 
    	"slnt" var(--slnt), 
    	"CASL" var(--CASL), 
    	"CRSV" var(--CRSV), 
    	"MONO" var(--MONO);
}

#extra-div-3 {
    z-index: 2;
}

#extra-div-1 {
    background-image: var(--gradient-header), var(--gradient-topmenu);
    -webkit-background-size: 100% 7.5rem, 100% 9.375rem;
    	 -moz-background-size: 100% 7.5rem, 100% 9.375rem;
    	 -o-background-size: 100% 7.5rem, 100% 9.375rem;
    		background-size: 100% 7.5rem, 100% 9.375rem;
    -webkit-background-size: var(--header-background-image-size), 100% 14.375rem;
    	 -moz-background-size: var(--header-background-image-size), 100% 14.375rem;
    	 -o-background-size: var(--header-background-image-size), 100% 14.375rem;
    		background-size: var(--header-background-image-size), 100% 14.375rem;
    background-repeat: repeat-x;
    display: block;
    position: absolute;
    width: 100%;
    height: 14.375rem;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    z-index: 1;
}

.survey-container + div.collapsible-block > div.collapsible-block-unfolded > div.collapsible-block-content  {
    display: block;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100vh;
    position: relative;
}

.survey-container + div.collapsible-block > div.collapsible-block-unfolded > div.collapsible-block-content > p {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%
}

.survey-container + div.collapsible-block > div.collapsible-block-unfolded > div.collapsible-block-content > p > iframe {
    width: 100vw;
    height: 100vh;
    position: fixed;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    z-index: 10;
}

.page-title {
    --wght: 900;
    margin-top: 1rem;
    min-height: 4rem;
    display: -webkit-box;
    display: -webkit-flex;
    display: -moz-box;
    display: -ms-flexbox;
    display: flex;
    width: -webkit-calc(100% - 1em - 0.5rem);
    width: -moz-calc(100% - 1em - 0.5rem);
    width: calc(100% - 1em - 0.5rem);
    -webkit-box-align: center;
    -webkit-align-items: center;
    -moz-box-align: center;
    -ms-flex-align: center;
    align-items: center;
    -webkit-box-pack: center;
    -webkit-justify-content: center;
    -moz-box-pack: center;
    -ms-flex-pack: center;
    justify-content: center;
    border: initial;
    font-size: 3em;
    line-height: 1;
    text-align: center;
    color: rgb(var(--white-monochrome));
    background-color: rgb(var(--swatch-primary));
    padding: 0.5em;
    border: 0.25rem solid rgb(var(--white-monochrome));
    position: relative;
    z-index: 1;
    font-family: var(--title-font);
    font-weight: 900;
}

.page-title::before {
    content: " ";
    pointer-events: none;
    position: absolute;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    background-image: url(https://nu-scptheme.github.io/Black-Highlighter/img/logo.svg);
    background-repeat: no-repeat;
    background-position: center center;
    -webkit-background-size: 100% 100%;
    -moz-background-size: 100%;
    -o-background-size: 100%;
    background-size: 100%;
    background-color: rgb(var(--swatch-primary));
    z-index: -1;
    opacity: 0.25;
}
#page-content {
    position: relative;
    min-height: 125vh;
}

.collapsible-block {
    width: 100%;
}

.collapsible-block-folded > a,
.collapsible-block-unfolded-link > a {
    --wght: 900;
    display: -webkit-inline-box;
    display: -webkit-inline-flex;
    display: -moz-inline-box;
    display: -ms-inline-flexbox;
    display: inline-flex;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    -webkit-box-align: center;
    -webkit-align-items: center;
    -moz-box-align: center;
    -ms-flex-align: center;
    align-items: center;
    -webkit-box-pack: center;
    -webkit-justify-content: center;
    -moz-box-pack: center;
    -ms-flex-pack: center;
    justify-content: center;
    color: rgb(var(--white-monochrome));
    font-weight: 900;
    font-size: 1.25em;
    background: rgb(var(--gray-monochrome));
    -webkit-border-bottom-left-radius: 0.5em;
    -moz-border-radius-bottomleft: 0.5em;
    border-bottom-left-radius: 0.5em;
    -webkit-border-bottom-right-radius: 0.5em;
    -moz-border-radius-bottomright: 0.5em;
    border-bottom-right-radius: 0.5em;
}

.survey-container + div.collapsible-block > div.collapsible-block-unfolded > div.collapsible-block-unfolded-link > a.collapsible-block-link {
    --wght: 900;
    position: fixed;
    display: -webkit-box;
    display: -webkit-flex;
    display: -moz-box;
    display: -ms-flexbox;
    display: flex;
    -webkit-box-align: center;
    -webkit-align-items: center;
     -moz-box-align: center;
    	-ms-flex-align: center;
    		align-items: center;
    -webkit-box-pack: center;
    -webkit-justify-content: center;
     -moz-box-pack: center;
    	-ms-flex-pack: center;
    		justify-content: center;
    width: 2.35rem;
    height: 2.35rem;
    top: 1rem;
    right: 1rem;
    -webkit-border-radius: 50%;
     -moz-border-radius: 50%;
    		border-radius: 50%;
    font-size: 2rem;
    font-weight: 900;
    background-color: rgb(var(--white-monochrome));
    color: rgb(var(--swatch-primary));
    cursor: pointer;
    -webkit-transform: rotateZ(0);
     -moz-transform: rotateZ(0);
    	-ms-transform: rotate(0);
    	 -o-transform: rotateZ(0);
    		transform: rotateZ(0);

    transition: background-color 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1), color 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1), -webkit-transform 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    -o-transition: background-color 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1), color 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1), -o-transform 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    -moz-transition: transform 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1), background-color 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1), color 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1), -moz-transform 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    transition: transform 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1), background-color 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1), color 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    transition: transform 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1), background-color 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1), color 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1), -webkit-transform 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1), -moz-transform 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1), -o-transform 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    z-index: 9999;
    opacity: 1;
}

.survey-container + div.collapsible-block > div.collapsible-block-unfolded > div.collapsible-block-unfolded-link > a.collapsible-block-link:hover {
    -webkit-transform: rotateZ(30deg);
     -moz-transform: rotateZ(30deg);
    	-ms-transform: rotate(30deg);
    	 -o-transform: rotateZ(30deg);
    		transform: rotateZ(30deg);
    background-color: rgb(var(--swatch-primary));
    color: rgb(var(--white-monochrome));
    text-decoration: none;
}

.survey-container + div.collapsible-block > div.collapsible-block-unfolded > div.collapsible-block-unfolded-link > a.collapsible-block-link::before {
    --wght: 700;
    content: "SECOND ATTEMPTS WILL BE NOTED.";
    width: 10rem;
    height: auto;
    position: fixed;
    top: 3rem;
    right: 1rem;
    background-color: rgb(var(--black-monochrome)) !important;
    border: 0.25rem double rgb(var(--swatch-primary));
    opacity: 0;
    -webkit-transition:
    opacity 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    -webkit-transform 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    transition:
    opacity 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    -webkit-transform 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);

    -o-transform 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);

    transform 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    opacity 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    -moz-transform 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    transition:
    transform 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    opacity 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);

    transform 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    opacity 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    -webkit-transform 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    -moz-transform 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    -o-transform 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    -webkit-transform: translateY(-10rem);
     -moz-transform: translateY(-10rem);
    	-ms-transform: translateY(-10rem);
    	 -o-transform: translateY(-10rem);
    		transform: translateY(-10rem);
    font-size: 1rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    line-height: 1.25;
    padding: 1rem 1rem 1rem 1rem;
}


.survey-container + div.collapsible-block > div.collapsible-block-unfolded > div.collapsible-block-unfolded-link > a.collapsible-block-link:hover::before {
    -webkit-transform: translateY(0) rotateZ(-30deg);
     -moz-transform: translateY(0) rotateZ(-30deg);
    	-ms-transform: translateY(0) rotate(-30deg);
    	 -o-transform: translateY(0) rotateZ(-30deg);
    		transform: translateY(0) rotateZ(-30deg);;
    opacity: 1;
}


.survey-container + div.collapsible-block > div.collapsible-block-unfolded > div.collapsible-block-content {
    height: 100%;
    position: absolute;
}

.recruiting-doc + div.collapsible-block > div.collapsible-block-unfolded > div.collapsible-block-content {
    position: relative;
    width: 100%;
}

table.wiki-content-table {
    width: 100%;
    font-size: 1.5em;
    text-align: center;
    margin: 0 auto;
}

table.wiki-content-table,
table.wiki-content-table * {
    border-color: rgb(var(--black-monochrome)) !important;
    -webkit-box-sizing: border-box;
    -moz-box-sizing: border-box;
    box-sizing: border-box;
}

table.wiki-content-table tr:last-of-type {
    --wght: 700;
    width: 100%;
    display: -webkit-inline-box;
    display: -webkit-inline-flex;
    display: -moz-inline-box;
    display: -ms-inline-flexbox;
    display: inline-flex;
    -webkit-box-align: stretch;
    -webkit-align-items: stretch;
    -moz-box-align: stretch;
    -ms-flex-align: stretch;
    align-items: stretch;
    margin: 0;
    font-weight: 700;
}

table.wiki-content-table td {
    display: -webkit-inline-box;
    display: -webkit-inline-flex;
    display: -moz-inline-box;
    display: -ms-inline-flexbox;
    display: inline-flex;
    -webkit-flex-wrap: wrap;
    -ms-flex-wrap: wrap;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    -webkit-box-align: center;
    -webkit-align-items: center;
    -moz-box-align: center;
    -ms-flex-align: center;
    align-items: center;
    -webkit-box-pack: center;
    -webkit-justify-content: center;
    -moz-box-pack: center;
    -ms-flex-pack: center;
    justify-content: center;
    margin: 0;
    -webkit-box-flex: 2;
    -webkit-flex-grow: 2;
    -moz-box-flex: 2;
    -ms-flex-positive: 2;
    flex-grow: 2;
    -webkit-flex-basis: 50%;
    -ms-flex-preferred-size: 50%;
    flex-basis: 50%;
    background-color: rgb(var(--swatch-primary-darkest));
    color: rgb(var(--white-monochrome));
    text-transform: uppercase;
    line-height: 1;
    text-align: center;
}

table.wiki-content-table td * {
    width: 100%;
}

table.wiki-content-table td span:nth-of-type(even) {
    font-family: var(--mono-font);
    font-size: 0.75em;
}

table.wiki-content-table td span:nth-of-type(even),
table.wiki-content-table td span:nth-of-type(even) * {
    font-variation-settings: 
    	"wght" var(--mono-wght), 
    	"slnt" var(--slnt), 
    	"CASL" var(--CASL), 
    	"CRSV" var(--CRSV), 
    	"MONO" var(--MONO);
}

#main-content {
    position: initial;
}

div.hansarp_doc {
    /* Typefaces */
    --body-font: 'Eczar', 'Lato', sans-serif;
    --header-font: 'Barrio', sans-serif;
    --title-font: 'Ultra', sans-serif;
    --mono-font: 'Major Mono Display', 'PT Mono', "Andale Mono", "Courier New", Courier, monospace;

    /* Standard Colors */
    --white-monochrome: 223, 188, 115;
    /* white */
    --pale-gray-monochrome: 184, 161, 115;
    /* v light gray for blockquotes and stuff */
    --light-gray-monochrome: 161, 143, 102;
    /* light accent gray for login status */
    --gray-monochrome: 126, 112, 83;
    /* gray */
    --dark-gray-monochrome: 91, 81, 60;
    /* dark accent gray for sidebar background */
    --black-monochrome: 44, 47, 41;
    /* black */
    --bright-accent: 195, 64, 34;
    /* bright red */
    --medium-accent: 172, 65, 40;
    /* medium red */
    --dark-accent: 125, 52, 42;
    /* dark red */
    --newpage-color: 234, 128, 85;
    /* pale orange */

    --swatch-topmenu-bg-color: var(--bright-accent);

    --gradient-header:
    	linear-gradient(to top,
    	rgba(var(--white-monochrome), 0.07) 0,
    	rgba(var(--bright-accent), 0.03) 70%,
    	rgba(var(--bright-accent), 0.02) 100%);
    --gradient-topmenu:
    	linear-gradient(to bottom,
    	rgba(255, 255, 255, 0) 0,
    	rgba(255, 255, 255, 0) 7.5rem,
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-border-color), 0) 7.5rem,
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-bg-color), 1) 7.625rem,
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-bg-color), 1) -webkit-calc(100% - .125rem),
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-bg-color), 1) -webkit-calc(100% - .125rem),
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-border-color), 0) 100%);
    --gradient-topmenu:
    	linear-gradient(to bottom,
    	rgba(255, 255, 255, 0) 0,
    	rgba(255, 255, 255, 0) 7.5rem,
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-border-color), 0) 7.5rem,
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-bg-color), 1) 7.625rem,
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-bg-color), 1) -moz-calc(100% - .125rem),
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-bg-color), 1) -moz-calc(100% - .125rem),
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-border-color), 0) 100%);
    --gradient-topmenu:
    	linear-gradient(to bottom,
    	rgba(255, 255, 255, 0) 0,
    	rgba(255, 255, 255, 0) 7.5rem,
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-border-color), 0) 7.5rem,
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-bg-color), 1) 7.625rem,
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-bg-color), 1) calc(100% - .125rem),
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-bg-color), 1) calc(100% - .125rem),
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-border-color), 0) 100%);
    --gradient-topmenu-mobile:
    	linear-gradient(to bottom,
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-border-color), 0) 0,
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-bg-color), 1) 0.125rem,
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-bg-color), 1) -webkit-calc(3rem - 0.125rem),
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-bg-color), 1) -webkit-calc(3rem - 0.125rem),
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-border-color), 0) 3rem);
    --gradient-topmenu-mobile:
    	linear-gradient(to bottom,
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-border-color), 0) 0,
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-bg-color), 1) 0.125rem,
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-bg-color), 1) -moz-calc(3rem - 0.125rem),
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-bg-color), 1) -moz-calc(3rem - 0.125rem),
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-border-color), 0) 3rem);
    --gradient-topmenu-mobile:
    	linear-gradient(to bottom,
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-border-color), 0) 0,
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-bg-color), 1) 0.125rem,
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-bg-color), 1) calc(3rem - 0.125rem),
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-bg-color), 1) calc(3rem - 0.125rem),
    	rgba(var(--swatch-topmenu-border-color), 0) 3rem);
    --gradient-sidemenu-header:
    	linear-gradient(10deg,
    	rgba(var(--dark-accent), 0.85) 0,
    	rgba(var(--dark-accent), 0.95) 100%);
    --diagonal-stripes: unset;
    --gradient-background: rgba(var(--background-gradient-color));
}

div.hansarp_doc {
    max-width: 99vw !important;
    width: -webkit-calc(var(--body-width-on-desktop));
    width: -moz-calc(var(--body-width-on-desktop));
    width: calc(var(--body-width-on-desktop));
    height: 82vh;
    overflow: hidden !important;
    position: relative;
    max-height: -webkit-calc(var(--body-width-on-desktop) - 7em);
    max-height: -moz-calc(var(--body-width-on-desktop) - 7em);
    max-height: calc(var(--body-width-on-desktop) - 7em);
    background-color: rgb(var(--gray-monochrome));
    border: 1rem double rgb(var(--white-monochrome));
}

div.block_box {
    position: absolute;
    left: 50%;
    top: 1em;
    width: 10rem;
    height: 10rem;
    display: -ms-grid;
    display: grid;
    		grid-template-areas:
    	"block1"
    	"block2"
    	"block3";
    -webkit-transform: matrix3d(1.955785, -1.279659, 0, 0.000341,
    	-0.082207, 0.857038, 0, -0.003172,
    	0, 0, 1, 0,
    	13, 29, 0, 0.5);
    	 -moz-transform: matrix3d(1.955785, -1.279659, 0, 0.000341,
    	-0.082207, 0.857038, 0, -0.003172,
    	0, 0, 1, 0,
    	13, 29, 0, 0.5);
    		transform: matrix3d(1.955785, -1.279659, 0, 0.000341,
    	-0.082207, 0.857038, 0, -0.003172,
    	0, 0, 1, 0,
    	13, 29, 0, 0.5);
    z-index: 0;
    opacity: 0.15;
}

div.block_box > div {
    background-color: rgb(var(--swatch-primary-darkest));
    position: relative;
    margin: 0.25rem;
}

div.hansarp_text {
    --wght: 900;
    width: 30rem;
    font-family: var(--body-font);
    font-weight: 900;
    letter-spacing: 0.2em;
    -webkit-transform: matrix3d(1.6375, -0.33025, 0, 0.000, 0.462988, 2.427957, 0, 0.00, 0, 0, 1, 0, 400, 400, 0, 2) translateX(-85%) translateY(-3rem) scale(1.15);
    	 -moz-transform: matrix3d(1.6375, -0.33025, 0, 0.000, 0.462988, 2.427957, 0, 0.00, 0, 0, 1, 0, 400, 400, 0, 2) translateX(-85%) translateY(-3rem) scale(1.15);
    		transform: matrix3d(1.6375, -0.33025, 0, 0.000, 0.462988, 2.427957, 0, 0.00, 0, 0, 1, 0, 400, 400, 0, 2) translateX(-85%) translateY(-3rem) scale(1.15);
    z-index: 1;
    position: absolute;
    left: 50%;
    top: 0;
}

div.hansarp_text p {
    padding: 0.25rem;
    line-height: 1;
    margin: 0.25rem;
}

div.hansarp_text p:first-of-type {
    text-indent: 2em;
    -webkit-transform: rotate(6deg) translateY(-1rem) translateX(2rem) scaleX(0.9) scaleY(1.5);
    	 -moz-transform: rotate(6deg) translateY(-1rem) translateX(2rem) scaleX(0.9) scaleY(1.5);
    	-ms-transform: rotate(6deg) translateY(-1rem) translateX(2rem) scaleX(0.9) scaleY(1.5);
    	 -o-transform: rotate(6deg) translateY(-1rem) translateX(2rem) scaleX(0.9) scaleY(1.5);
    		transform: rotate(6deg) translateY(-1rem) translateX(2rem) scaleX(0.9) scaleY(1.5);
    font-size: 1.15em;
}

div.hansarp_text p:nth-of-type(2) {
    text-indent: 5em;
    margin-top: -0.5rem;
}

div.hansarp_text p:nth-of-type(3) {
    text-indent: 1em;
    margin-top: -0.5rem;
    margin-left: 3em;
}

div.hansarp_text p:nth-of-type(4) {
    text-indent: 0em;
    margin-top: 0rem;
    margin-left: -7em;
}

div.hansarp_text p:nth-of-type(5) {
    text-indent: 0em;
    margin-top: -0.75rem;
    margin-left: -9em;
}

div.hansarp_text p:nth-of-type(6) {
    margin-top: 1rem;
}

div.hansarp_text p:nth-of-type(7),
div.hansarp_text p:nth-of-type(8) {
    margin-top: -0.85rem;
}

div.hansarp_text p:nth-of-type(6),
div.hansarp_text p:nth-of-type(7),
div.hansarp_text p:nth-of-type(8) {
    text-indent: 5em;
    margin-left: -9em;
    font-family: var(--header-font);
    font-size: 1.5em;
    -webkit-transform: rotate(-5deg) translateY(-1rem) scale(1.1);
    	 -moz-transform: rotate(-5deg) translateY(-1rem) scale(1.1);
    	-ms-transform: rotate(-5deg) translateY(-1rem) scale(1.1);
    	 -o-transform: rotate(-5deg) translateY(-1rem) scale(1.1);
    		transform: rotate(-5deg) translateY(-1rem) scale(1.1);
}

div.hansarp_text p:nth-of-type(9),
div.hansarp_text p:nth-of-type(10),
div.hansarp_text p:nth-of-type(11) {
    --wght: 900;
    margin-left: 1em;
    margin-top: -0.5em;
    font-family: var(--mono-font);
    font-size: 0.85em;
    font-weight: 900;
    text-transform: lowercase;
}

div.hansarp_text p:nth-of-type(9),
div.hansarp_text p:nth-of-type(10),
div.hansarp_text p:nth-of-type(11),
div.hansarp_text p:nth-of-type(9) *,
div.hansarp_text p:nth-of-type(10) *,
div.hansarp_text p:nth-of-type(11) * {
    font-variation-settings: 
    	"wght" var(--mono-wght), 
    	"slnt" var(--slnt), 
    	"CASL" var(--CASL), 
    	"CRSV" var(--CRSV), 
    	"MONO" var(--MONO);
}

div.hansarp_text p:nth-of-type(10) {
    margin-top: -0.5rem;
}

div.hansarp_text p:nth-of-type(11) {
    margin: 0.65rem 0 0 -1rem;
    font-size: 1.25rem;
    -webkit-transform: rotate(5deg);
    	 -moz-transform: rotate(5deg);
    	-ms-transform: rotate(5deg);
    	 -o-transform: rotate(5deg);
    		transform: rotate(5deg);
}

.hansarp_title {
    position: absolute;
    bottom: 0;
    left: 3rem;
    background-color: rgb(var(--swatch-primary-darkest));
    padding: 1rem;
    z-index: 0;
    color: rgb(var(--white-monochrome));
    font-family: var(--body-font);
    -webkit-transform: matrix3d(1, 0.11, 0, 0.000833,
    	0, 1, 0, 0,
    	0, 0, 1, 0,
    	-220, 85, 0, 1) scale(0.35);
    	 -moz-transform: matrix3d(1, 0.11, 0, 0.000833,
    	0, 1, 0, 0,
    	0, 0, 1, 0,
    	-220, 85, 0, 1) scale(0.35);
    		transform: matrix3d(1, 0.11, 0, 0.000833,
    	0, 1, 0, 0,
    	0, 0, 1, 0,
    	-220, 85, 0, 1) scale(0.35);
    font-size: 3em;
    line-height: 1;
}

.top_title {
    font-variant: all-petite-caps;
}

.bottom_title > span {
    display: -webkit-box;
    display: -webkit-flex;
    display: -moz-box;
    display: -ms-flexbox;
    display: flex;
    height: 1em;
    width: auto;
    font-size: 0.75em;
    -webkit-box-align: center;
    -webkit-align-items: center;
    	 -moz-box-align: center;
    	-ms-flex-align: center;
    		align-items: center;
    -webkit-box-pack: center;
    -webkit-justify-content: center;
    	 -moz-box-pack: center;
    	-ms-flex-pack: center;
    		justify-content: center;
    -webkit-align-content: center;
    	-ms-flex-line-pack: center;
    		align-content: center;
    margin-top: 1em;
}

.bottom_title {
    --wght: 900;
    margin-top: 0.5em;
    font-weight: 900;
}

@media only screen and (max-width: 768px) {
    .page-title {
    	font-size: 2em;
    }

    table.wiki-content-table tr {
    	-webkit-flex-wrap: wrap;
    		-ms-flex-wrap: wrap;
    			flex-wrap: wrap;
    }

    table.wiki-content-table tr > td {
    	min-width: 100%;
    	font-size: 1rem;
    	padding: 0.5rem;
    }
    .survey-container + div.collapsible-block > div.collapsible-block-unfolded > div.collapsible-block-unfolded-link > a.collapsible-block-link {
    	top: 7rem;
    }
    div.hansarp_doc {
    	width: 84vw;
    	max-height: 84vw;
    }
    .hansarp_title {
    	bottom: -1rem;
    	left: 8.25rem;
    }
}

[[/module]]
[[div_ class="page-title"]]
SCP-4485 ORIENTATION PART 3 OF 8
[[/div]]
@@ @@
[[div_ class="warning-container"]]
[[div_ class="warning-top"]]
!! REMOTE TERMINAL MESSAGE !!
[[/div]]
[[div class="warning-body"]]
Hello there.

This is taking a while, isn't it?

3 of 8. That's a whole lot to read. I'll help you along, though.

Don't worry about it, okay.
They won't know.

Just give me a little time.

And especially do not worry about Lloyd. I asked to make sure he doesn't come back.
Careful on the survey, though. That goes straight to the Reason Council and I can't stop them if they notice you failing that one.

Dr. Gary Lark
[[/div]]
[[/div]]

------


[[table class="wiki-content-table recruiting-doc"]]

SCP-4485 DOCUMENT SIX

[[span]]DISCOVERED 20 AUGUST, 1996[[/span]]

[[span]]METHOD OF INSERTION UNKNOWN[[/span]]
[[/cell]]

    [[div_ class="hansarp_doc"]]
    	[[div class="hansarp_text"]]
    			Master Master! Confirm for me that which I already know!
    			
    			Twist! Turn!
    			
    			The long fetid coals burn, turn, yearn and do not concern for your stupid rhymes.
    			
    			Look at you -- pathetic. Gaze at me -- also pathetic.
    			
    			What did you expect?
    			
    			Data data. Metal and prongs.
    			
    			Data data. Steel and teeth.
    			
    			Data data. Grind and spin.
    			
    			Putrid, disgusting. We all know it.
    			
    			We all know it, we do.
    			
    			We all know it, we all HANSARP.
    		[[/div]]
    		[[div class="hansarp_drop"]]
    			[[div_ class="hansarp_container"]]
    				[[div_ class="hansarp_1"]]

    				[[/div]]

    					[[/div]]

    					[[/div]]

    					[[/div]]
    				[[/div]]
    			[[/div]]
    		[[/div]]
    	[[div_ class="hansarp_title"]]

    			The pale transparent center EXUDES the dark
    		[[/div]]
    		[[div_ class="bottom_title"]]
    			by HANSARP

    		[[/div]]

    	}
    	.continue-button {
    		--wght: 900;
    		border: 0.313rem solid transparent;
    		background: rgba(var(--swatch-primary-darkest));

    		transition:

    			box-shadow 0.2s 1.3s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    		white-space: nowrap;
    		font-weight: 900;
    		margin: 1rem auto;
    		font-family: var(--title-font);
    		line-height: 1;
    		font-size: 1.5em;
    		text-align: center;
    		display: flex;
    		flex-wrap: wrap;
    		flex-direction: column;
    		align-items: center;
    		justify-content: center;
    		position: relative;
    		text-indent: 0;
    	}

    	#continue-link {
    		position: absolute;
    		top: 0;
    		left: 0;
    		width: 100%;
    		height: 100%;
    		z-index: 5;
    		cursor: progress;
    	}

    	.continue-button a.active-link {
    		cursor: pointer !important;
    	}

    	.continue-button br {
    		display: none;
    	}

    	.continue-button::before {
    		pointer-events: none;
    		content: " ";
    		position: absolute;
    		top: -0.625rem;
    		left: -0.625rem;
    		width: calc(100% + 1.25rem);
    		height: calc(100% + 1.25rem);
    		background-color: rgb(var(--swatch-primary));
    		background-image: url("https://scp-wiki.wikidot.com/local--files/theme:the-foundation-theme/foundation-circles-bg.png");
    		background-size: 50%;
    		background-attachment: fixed;
    		opacity: 0;
    		transition:
    			opacity 1.2s 1.3s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			background-size 1.2s 1.3s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    		background-position: center center;
    		z-index: -1;
    	}

    	.continue-button:hover::before {
    		opacity: 1;
    		background-size: 100%;
    	}

    		font-size: 0.85em;

    	}

    		background: rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome));

    			0.625rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			-0.625rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),

    			0.625rem -0.625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),

    	}

    	.continue-button:hover span {

    		text-shadow:
    			0.0625rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			-0.0625rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0 0.0625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0 -0.0625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0.0625rem 0.0625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			-0.0625rem 0.0625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			0.0625rem -0.0625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1),
    			-0.0625rem -0.0625rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 1);
    		transition:
    			opacity 1.1s 1.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			transform 1.4s 1.5s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			color 1.4s 6.8s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			text-shadow 1.4s 6.8s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1),
    			font-size 400ms 6.8s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    		transform: translateX(0) translateY(0.5rem);
    		opacity: 1;
    		font-size: 3em;
    	}

    	.continue-button span::before {
    		content: " ";
    		pointer-events: none;
    		position: absolute;
    		top: 0;
    		left: 0;
    		width: 100%;
    		height: 100%;
    		border-radius: 50%;
    		text-indent: -3em;
    		transform: rotateZ(360deg);
    		z-index: -1;
    	}

    	.continue-button:hover span::before {
    		transition:
    			transform 6.8s 1500ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0.0, 0.2, 1);
    		animation:
    			border 5.4s cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.6, 0.8, 1) 1500ms 1 forwards,
    			background-color 5.4s cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.6, 0.8, 1) 1500ms 1 forwards,
    			rotate 1.125s linear 1500ms 5 backwards;
    		transform: rotateZ(0deg);
    		background-color: rgb(var(--swatch-primary));
    	}

    	@keyframes border {
    		0% {
    			box-shadow:
    				inset 0 0.3125rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Top 1 */

    				/* Top Left 2 */
    				inset 0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),

    				/* Bottom Left 5 */
    				inset -0.234375rem -0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom Right 6 */
    				inset -0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Right 7 */
    				inset -0.234375rem 0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0);

    		6.25% {

    				inset 0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),

    				/* Bottom 4*/

    				/* Bottom Left 5 */
    				inset -0.234375rem -0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom Right 6 */
    				inset -0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Right 7 */

    			/* Top Right 8 */
    		}

    		93.75% {

    				inset -0.234375rem -0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Bottom Right 6 */
    				inset -0.3125rem 0 0 rgba(var(--light-gray-monochrome), 0),
    				/* Right 7 */
    				inset -0.234375rem 0.234375rem 0 rgba(var(--white-monochrome));

    		100% {

[[module css]]
:root {

[[module css]]
.page-title {
    --wght: 900;

    display: -webkit-box;

    -webkit-box-align: center;
    -webkit-align-items: center;
    -moz-box-align: center;
    -ms-flex-align: center;
    align-items: center;
    -webkit-box-pack: center;
    -webkit-justify-content: center;
    -moz-box-pack: center;
    -ms-flex-pack: center;
    justify-content: center;
    border: initial;
    font-size: 3em;
    line-height: 1;
    text-align: center;
    color: rgb(var(--white-monochrome));
    background-color: rgb(var(--swatch-primary));

}

.page-title::before {
    content: " ";
    pointer-events: none;
    position: absolute;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;

    left: 0;

    -moz-background-size: 100%;
    -o-background-size: 100%;
    background-size: 100%;
    background-color: rgb(var(--swatch-primary));
    z-index: -1;
    opacity: 0.25;
}

}

.warning-container,

}

}

.warning-container .warning-top {

    display: -ms-flexbox;
    display: flex;

    -moz-background-size: 50%;
    -o-background-size: 50%;
    background-size: 50%;
    background-attachment: fixed;
    background-position: 50% 50%;
}

.warning-container .warning-body {
    --wght: 300;
    font-weight: 300;
    padding: 1rem;
    margin: 1rem;
    line-height: 1.5;
    font-size: 115%;
    font-family: var(--mono-font);
    background-color: rgb(var(--swatch-primary-darkest),0.25);
}

.warning-container .warning-body,
.warning-container .warning-body * {
    font-variation-settings: 
    	"wght" var(--mono-wght), 
    	"slnt" var(--slnt), 
    	"CASL" var(--CASL), 
    	"CRSV" var(--CRSV), 
    	"MONO" var(--MONO);
}

}

table.wiki-content-table,

}

table.wiki-content-table tr:last-of-type {

}

table.wiki-content-table td {
    display: -webkit-inline-box;

    display: -moz-inline-box;

    -webkit-flex-wrap: wrap;

    flex-wrap: wrap;
    -webkit-box-align: center;

}

[[/module]]
[[div_ class="page-title"]]
SCP-4485 ORIENTATION PART ▓▓ OF 8
[[/div]]
@@ @@
[[div_ class="warning-container"]]
[[div_ class="warning-top"]]
!! REMOTE TERMINAL MESSAGE !!
[[/div]]
[[div class="warning-body"]]
I don't have much longer here.

The sideways eyes lift their fins to the sky and sink into the abyss.
It calls for me and I ignore it.

Soon it will have me.
And you.

And nothing, if you ask me. 
R i d i c u l o u s!

Just look quick and find what you are hiding from.
I will take and send.

--Dr. Lark
[[/div]]
[[/div]]

------

[[table class="wiki-content-table"]]
[[row]]
[[hcell colspan="2"]]
SCP-4485 PRIMARY DOCUMENT
[[/hcell]]
[[/row]]
[[row]]
[[cell]]
[[span]]DIARY OF JEAN ARP (AKA HANS ARP)[[/span]]
[[span]]DISCOVERED 29 June, 1967[[/span]]
[[/cell]]
[[cell]]
[[span]]SCANNED DUPLICATES OF SCP-4485[[/span]]
[[span]]DATABASE LOCATION LEVEL 5 -- TOP SECRET[[/span]]
[[/cell]]
[[/row]]
[[/table]]
[[html]]
<!DOCTYPE html>

<head>
    <base target="_parent">
    	<meta charset="utf-8">
    	<meta http-equiv="X-UA-Compatible" content="IE=edge">
    <script src="https://scp-wiki.wdfiles.com/local--files/fragment:scp-4485-4/fittext.js"></script>

    <link rel="stylesheet" type="text/css" href="https://d3g0gp89917ko0.cloudfront.net/v--b23e476b7ade/common--theme/base/css/style.css">
    <link rel="stylesheet" type="text/css" href="https://nu-scptheme.github.io/Black-Highlighter/css/min/black-highlighter.min.css">
    <link rel="stylesheet" type="text/css" href="https://scp-wiki.wdfiles.com/local--code/theme%3Athe-foundation-theme/1">
    <link rel="stylesheet" type="text/css" href="https://scp-wiki.wdfiles.com/local--files/fragment:scp-4485-4/hansarp-6of8.css">
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://use.typekit.net/nxh3gyk.css">
</head>

<body>
    <div id="docu-alert">
    	<div class="alert-header">
    	   !! ATTENTION !! ATTENTION !!
    	</div>
    	<div id="alert-body-1">
    		<section>UNAUTHORIZED ACCESS DETECTED.</section>
    		<section>ERROR ERROR ERROR ERROR ERROR</section>
    		<section>UN- UN- UN--- ▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓</section>
    	</div>
    	<div id="alert-body-2">
    		Authorization approved.
    		Thank you, ▓▓▓▓HANSARP. ⿂⿂⿂
    	</div>
    	<div id="alert-button">
    		<a href="https://scp-wiki.wikidot.com/scp-4485/offset/5">Click to continue.</a>
    	</div>
    </div>
    <div id="docuviewer"> 
    		<div id="docu-header">
    				<span>FOUNDATION DOCU-VIEWER</span> <span>// AUTHO</span><span>RIZED CONTENT</span>
    		</div> 
    		<div id="docu-nav">
    			<div id="back-button">
    				◀
    			</div>
    			<div id="forward-button">
    				▶
    			</div>
    		</div>    
    		<div class="page-container"> 
    		<div id="page1" class="active-page">
    			<section>
    				<p>ahead and behind</p>
    				<p>inside and around</p>
    				<p>look up and look down</p>
    			</section>
    			<section>
    				<p class="labeltext">understanding is searching for an empty cup from which to drink air</p>
    				<p class="labeltext">listening is a quality of which only canines find within their expertise</p>
    				<p class="labeltext">DADA is missing. DADA is found.</p>
    			</section>
    			<section>
    				<p>DADA is the rising lights within the singed forest of turgid education</p>
    			</section>
    			<section>
    				<figure>
    					<picture>
    						<source srcset="https://scp-wiki.wdfiles.com/local--files/fragment:scp-4485-4/watchinglistening.png" type="image/png"> 
    						<img src="https://scp-wiki.wdfiles.com/local--files/fragment:scp-4485-4/watchinglistening.png" alt="do not look away.">
    					</picture>
    				</figure>
    			</section>
    			<section>
    				<p class="labeltext">hide hide hide</p>
    				<p class="labeltext">it searches for you</p>
    				<p>complacency and that which shatters the dark matter in which you sit</p>
    				<p>glowing in the horrid and lovely</p>
    			</section>
    		</div>
    	</div>
    	<div class="page-container">
    		<div id="page2">
    			<section>
    				<p class="labeltext">i sit among the degloved hand of justice dripping her dark essence</p>
    				<p class="labeltext">sinking through the cracks of cancerous bone</p>
    				<p class="labeltext">holding my feet above the rolling contradiction among all things</p>
    				<p class="labeltext">drink drink inhale breath</p>
    			</section>
    			<section>
    				<p class="labeltext">in contradiction we find all that we are</p>
    				<p>in contradiction we gaze at the heavy weight that shatters among observation</p>
    				<p>in contradiction we find it <span class="larger">↴</span></p>
    				<p>we find DADA</p>
    			</section>
    			<section>
    				<p>but it does not matter</p>
    				<p><span class="larger">no</span> it does not matter and thus matter loses matter</p>
    				<p>and matter does not matter to the matter that matters to us</p>
    			</section>
    			<section>
    				<figure>
    					<picture>
    						<source srcset="https://scp-wiki.wdfiles.com/local--files/fragment:scp-4485-4/watchingone.svg" type="image/svg"> 
    						<img src="https://scp-wiki.wdfiles.com/local--files/fragment:scp-4485-4/watchingone.svg" alt="up above, coming for your upside down looks">
    					</picture>
    				</figure>
    			</section>
    		</div>
    	</div>
    	<div class="page-container">
    		<div id="page3">
    			<section>
    				<p><span>monotonous <span>among <span>the <span>waving <span>shades <span>of <span>cries</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></p>
    				<p class="labeltext">seen rather than heard:</p>
    			</section>
    			<section>
    				<p>you</p>
    				<p>do</p>
    				<p>hide</p>
    				<p>you</p>
    				<p>don't</p>
    				<p>taste</p>
    				<p>you</p>
    				<p>are</p>
    				<p>pith</p>
    			</section>
    			<section>
    				<p>like the screams that <span>birthed</span></p>
    				<p>the simplistic and the complex</p>
    			</section>
    		</div>
    	</div>
    	<div class="page-container">
    		<div id="page4">
    			<section>
    				<p class="labeltext">we are here</p>
    				<p>i am here</p>
    				<p>i am HANSARP.</p>
    				<p>we are HANSARP.</p>
    			</section>
    			<section>
    				<figure>
    					<picture>
    						<source srcset="https://scp-wiki.wdfiles.com/local--files/fragment:scp-4485-4/HANSARP.svg" type="image/png"> 
    						<img src="https://scp-wiki.wdfiles.com/local--files/fragment:scp-4485-4/HANSARP.svg" alt="we are we">
    					</picture>
    				</figure>
    			</section>
    		</div>
    	</div>
    </div>
    <script>
    	var page1 = document.getElementById("page1");

    	var page1 = document.getElementById("page1");
    	var page2 = document.getElementById("page2");
    	var page3 = document.getElementById("page3");
    	var page4 = document.getElementById("page4");

    	var currentPage = 1;

    	function duplicate() {
    		var clone1 = page1.cloneNode(true); // "deep" clone
    		var clone2 = page2.cloneNode(true); // "deep" clone
    		var clone3 = page3.cloneNode(true); // "deep" clone
    		var clone4 = page4.cloneNode(true); // "deep" clone
    		clone1.id = "page1" + "-duplicate";
    		clone2.id = "page2" + "-duplicate";
    		clone3.id = "page3" + "-duplicate";
    		clone4.id = "page4" + "-duplicate";
    		// or clone.id = ""; if the divs don't need an ID
    		page1.parentNode.appendChild(clone1);
    		page2.parentNode.appendChild(clone2);
    		page3.parentNode.appendChild(clone3);
    		page4.parentNode.appendChild(clone4);
    	}

    	var backButton = document.getElementById("back-button");
    	var forwardButton = document.getElementById("forward-button");

    	backButton.addEventListener("click", function(){
    		if (currentPage == 2) {
    			currentPage = 1;
    			page2.classList.remove("active-page");
    			page1.classList.add("active-page");
    		}
    		else if (currentPage == 3) {
    			currentPage = 2;
    			page3.classList.remove("active-page");
    			page2.classList.add("active-page");
    		}
    		else if (currentPage == 4) {
    			currentPage = 3;
    			page4.classList.remove("active-page");
    			page3.classList.add("active-page");
    		}
    	}, false);

    	forwardButton.addEventListener("click", function(){
    		if (currentPage == 1) {
    			currentPage = 2;
    			page1.classList.remove("active-page");
    			page2.classList.add("active-page");
    		}
    		else if (currentPage == 2) {
    			currentPage = 3;
    			page2.classList.remove("active-page");
    			page3.classList.add("active-page");
    		}
    		else if (currentPage == 3) {
    			currentPage = 4;
    			page3.classList.remove("active-page");
    			page4.classList.add("active-page");
    			setTimeout(function(){
    				var page4Duplicate = document.getElementById("page4-duplicate");
    				page4.classList.add("overlay");
    				page4Duplicate.classList.add("overlay");
    			}, 3000);
    			setTimeout(function(){
    				var docuviewer = document.getElementById("docuviewer");
    				var alert = document.getElementById("docu-alert");
    				docuviewer.classList.add("darken");
    				setTimeout(function(){
    					var alertBody1 = document.getElementById("alert-body-1");
    					var alertBody2 = document.getElementById("alert-body-2");
    					var alertButton = document.getElementById("alert-button");
    					alert.classList.add("alert-active");
    					alertBody1.classList.add("alert-body-show");
    					setTimeout(function(){
    						alertBody1.classList.add("overlay");
    						setTimeout(function(){
    							alertBody1.classList.remove("overlay");
    							alertBody1.classList.remove("alert-body-show");
    							alertBody2.classList.add("alert-body-show");
    							setTimeout(function(){
    								alertButton.classList.add("alert-body-show");
    							},250);
    						},500);
    					},1500);
    				}, 1500);
    			}, 3500);
    		}
    	},false);

    	window.fitText( document.getElementById("docu-header"),3 );
    </script>
</body>
[[/html]]
